def comms_send(channel, msg):
    sent = comms.send(channel, msg)
    print(sent)
    if sent.status != "ok":
        print(sent.message)



def rebirth(self, creature):

    self.input.connect('feed_maker_1')
    self.reagents.connect('inventory')

    print(self.set_revival_target(creature))

    #в инпут
    print(self.required_feed())
    if self.input.count() < 4:
        get_feed(self)
        #self.input.take(self.required_feed(), 10)

    for item, count in creature_revive_reagents[creature].items():
        print(item, count)
        shop.buy(item, quantity=count)
        self.reagents.take(item, count)

    print(self.revive())
    print(self.is_established() )

def get_feed(self):
    print('get_feed')
    self.input.connect('feed_maker_1')
    self.reagents.connect('inventory')

    _feed = self.required_feed()
    _recipe = f'craft_{_feed}'

    print('get_feed send:', _recipe)
    comms_send(f'jobs.feed_maker_1', {'recipe': _recipe})

    feed_maker_1 = get_component('feed_maker_1')

    print('wait feed_maker_1 ...')

    while True:

        feed_maker_stacks = ioslots.stacks_to_dict(feed_maker_1.output.stacks())
        print(feed_maker_stacks)
        if feed_maker_stacks.get(_feed, 0) < 20:
            sleep(5)
        else:
            ioslots.input_take(self, _feed, feed_maker_stacks[_feed])
            break



def food_inspection(self):
    while True:
        if self.input.count() < 5:
            print('заказ еды')
            get_feed(self)
        else:
            sleep(30)



def habitat_live_and_prosper11111(self):
    print('habitat_live_and_prosper')

    while True:

        required_gas = self.required_gas()
        if required_gas:
            #конннект газа
            if self.gas_in.connected_id() != storages_exotic.container[required_gas]:
                print(self.gas_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[required_gas]))
            #заливка газа до band_middle
            gas_band = self.gas_band()
            gas_band_middle = gas_band[1] - gas_band[0]
            #print(band_middle)
            while self.gas_level() < gas_band_middle + 50:
                self.set_gas_intake(100)
                sleep(1)
            self.set_gas_intake(0)


        required_liquid = self.required_liquid()
        if required_liquid:
            #конннект жидкости
            if self.liquid_in.connected_id() != storages_exotic.container[required_liquid]:
                print(self.liquid_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[required_liquid]))
            #заливка жидкости
            liquid_band = self.liquid_band()
            liquid_band_middle = liquid_band[1] - liquid_band[0]
            while self.liquid_level() < liquid_band_middle + 50:
                self.set_liquid_intake(100)
                sleep(1)
            self.set_liquid_intake(0)

        sleep(10)


def habitat_live_and_prosper(self):
    print('habitat_live_and_prosper')

    print(self.input.connect('large_warehouse_14'))

    while True:


        input_count = self.input.count()
        if input_count < 30:
            #print('input_take')
            ioslots.input_take(self, self.required_feed(), 50 - input_count)


        required_gas = self.required_gas()
        if required_gas:
            #print('required_gas')
            #конннект газа
            if self.gas_in.connected_id() != storages_exotic.container[required_gas]:
                print(self.purge_intake('gas_in'))
                print(self.purge_reserve("gas"))
                print(self.gas_in.disconnect())
                print(self.gas_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[required_gas]))
            #заливка газа до band_middle
            gas_band = self.gas_band()
            gas_band_middle = gas_band[1] - gas_band[0]
            #print(gas_band_middle)
            while self.gas_level() < gas_band[0] + (gas_band_middle/2) + 50:
                self.set_gas_intake(100)
                sleep(1)
            self.set_gas_intake(0)


        required_liquid = self.required_liquid()
        if required_liquid:
            #print('required_liquid')
            #конннект жидкости
            if self.liquid_in.connected_id() != storages_exotic.container[required_liquid]:
                print(self.purge_intake('liquid_in'))
                print(self.purge_reserve("liquid"))
                print(self.liquid_in.disconnect())
                print(self.liquid_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[required_liquid]))
            #заливка жидкости
            liquid_band = self.liquid_band()
            liquid_band_middle = liquid_band[1] - liquid_band[0]
            while self.liquid_level() < liquid_band_middle + 50:
                self.set_liquid_intake(100)
                sleep(1)
            self.set_liquid_intake(0)

        sleep(1)




def habitat_comm(self):
    print('habitat_comm ')

    print(self.input.connect('large_warehouse_14'))

    while True:

        input_count = self.input.count()
        if input_count < 30:

            large_warehouse_14 = get_component('large_warehouse_14')
            _feed = self.required_feed()

            if large_warehouse_14.count(_feed):
                self.input.connect('large_warehouse_14')
                ioslots.input_take(self, _feed, 20)

            #print('input_take')
            #ioslots.input_take(self, self.required_feed(), 50 - input_count)

            else:
                comms_send(f'jobs.feed', self.id)

                clock_start = clock.elapsed_game_hours()

                double_feed = False

                while True:

                    #if input_count < 10:
                        #comms_send(f'jobs.feed', self.id)
                        #double_feed = True


                    prev_input_count = input_count
                    input_count = self.input.count()
                    if input_count > prev_input_count:
                        print('feed приход')
                        self.clear_status()
                        break

                    clock_delta =  round((clock.elapsed_game_hours() - clock_start) * clock.real_seconds_per_hour())
                    self.set_status(f'req timer: {clock_delta} sec')
                    if clock_delta > 120:
                        print('feed приход скип по таймеру ')
                        break
                    sleep(1)


        required_gas = self.required_gas()
        if required_gas:
            #print('required_gas')
            #конннект газа
            if self.gas_in.connected_id() != storages_exotic.container[required_gas]:
                print(self.purge_intake('gas_in'))
                print(self.purge_reserve("gas"))
                print(self.gas_in.disconnect())
                print(self.gas_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[required_gas]))
            #заливка газа до band_middle
            gas_band = self.gas_band()
            gas_band_middle = gas_band[1] - gas_band[0]
            #print(gas_band_middle)

            if self.gas_level() < gas_band[0] + 10:
                while self.gas_level() < gas_band[0] + (gas_band_middle/2):
                    self.set_gas_intake(100)
                    print('set_gas_intake')
                    sleep(1)
            self.set_gas_intake(0)


        required_liquid = self.required_liquid()
        if required_liquid:
            #print('required_liquid')
            #конннект жидкости
            if self.liquid_in.connected_id() != storages_exotic.container[required_liquid]:
                print(self.purge_intake('liquid_in'))
                print(self.purge_reserve("liquid"))
                print(self.liquid_in.disconnect())
                print(self.liquid_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[required_liquid]))
            #заливка жидкости
            liquid_band = self.liquid_band()
            liquid_band_middle = liquid_band[1] - liquid_band[0]

            if self.liquid_level() < liquid_band[1] + 10:
                while self.liquid_level() < liquid_band[0] + (liquid_band_middle/2):
                    self.set_liquid_intake(100)
                    print('set_liquid_intake')
                    sleep(1)
            self.set_liquid_intake(0)

        sleep(1)







def feed_task(self):
    count = self.input.count()
    if count >= 30:
        return

    feed = self.required_feed()
    if get_component('large_warehouse_14').count(feed):
        self.input.connect('large_warehouse_14')
        ioslots.input_take(self, feed, 20)
        return

    comms_send('jobs.feed', self.id)
    extra_sent = False
    start = clock.elapsed_game_hours()

    while True:
        prev, count = count, self.input.count()

        if count > prev:
            print('feed приход')
            self.clear_status()
            return

        if count < 10 and not extra_sent:
            comms_send('jobs.feed', self.id)
            extra_sent = True
            print('feed доп. запрос')

        waited = round((clock.elapsed_game_hours() - start) * clock.real_seconds_per_hour())
        self.set_status(f'req timer: {waited} sec')
        if waited > 120:
            print('feed приход скип по таймеру')
            return
        sleep(1)


def gas_task(self):
    gas = self.required_gas()
    if not gas:
        return

    if self.gas_in.connected_id() != storages_exotic.container[gas]:
        print(self.purge_intake('gas_in'))
        print(self.purge_reserve('gas'))
        print(self.gas_in.disconnect())
        print(self.gas_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[gas]))

    low, high = self.gas_band()
    target = low + (high - low) / 2

    if self.gas_level() < low + 10:
        while self.gas_level() < target:
            self.set_gas_intake(100)
            print('set_gas_intake')
            sleep(1)
    self.set_gas_intake(0)


def liquid_task(self):
    liquid = self.required_liquid()
    if not liquid:
        return

    if self.liquid_in.connected_id() != storages_exotic.container[liquid]:
        print(self.purge_intake('liquid_in'))
        print(self.purge_reserve('liquid'))
        print(self.liquid_in.disconnect())
        print(self.liquid_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[liquid]))

    low, high = self.liquid_band()
    target = low + (high - low) / 2

    if self.liquid_level() < high + 10:
        while self.liquid_level() < target:
            self.set_liquid_intake(100)
            print('set_liquid_intake')
            sleep(1)
    self.set_liquid_intake(0)


def habitat_comm3333(self):
    print('habitat_comm ')
    print(self.input.connect('large_warehouse_14'))

    while True:
        feed_task(self)
        gas_task(self)
        liquid_task(self)
        sleep(1)

common
magmatic_annelid common 1 Только для этого вида увеличивает количество потомства за завершённые циклы размножения на 15 %.
magmatic_annelid common 2 Для всех видов увеличивает количество потомства за завершённые циклы размножения на 5 %.
salt_tortoise common 1 Только для этого вида снижает расход корма на каждую новую особь на 60 %.
salt_tortoise common 2 Для всех видов при численности менее 2 500 особей повышает скорость размножения на 15 %.

uncommon
veil_mantle uncommon 1 Только для этого вида повышает скорость размножения на 30 %.
veil_mantle uncommon 2 Для всех видов повышает скорость размножения на 10 %.
glasswing_mantis uncommon 1 Только для этого вида повышает скорость размножения на 30 %.
glasswing_mantis uncommon 2 Для всех видов повышает скорость размножения на 10 %.
mycelial_husk uncommon 1 Только для этого вида снижает расход корма на каждую новую особь на 40 % и расширяет диапазоны допустимого количества газа и жидкости на 25 %.
mycelial_husk uncommon 2 Для всех видов повышает скорость размножения на 1 % за каждый другой прижившийся вид, до 12 %.
mantle_strider uncommon 1 Только для этого вида при численности менее 2 500 особей повышает скорость размножения на 35 %.
mantle_strider uncommon 2 Для всех видов повышает предел естественной скорости размножения при большой численности на 10 %.

rare
ferric_sea_lily rare 1 Только для этого вида повышает скорость размножения на 30 %.
ferric_sea_lily rare 2 Для всех видов повышает скорость размножения на 10 %.
tidal_cephalopod rare 1 Только для этого вида позволяет использовать базовую жидкость на поздних стадиях и расширяет диапазон её допустимого количества на 25 %.
tidal_cephalopod rare 2 Для всех видов при численности более 10 000 особей повышает скорость размножения на 10 %.
bone_walker rare 1 Только для этого вида: на поздних стадиях жизни сохраняются базовые газ и жидкость вместо перехода на вещества, требуемые на высших стадиях.
bone_walker rare 2 Для всех видов: увеличивает скорость размножения на 1,5 % за каждую завершённую стадию жизни, максимум на 6 %.
vent_drifter rare 1 Только для этого вида позволяет использовать базовый газ на поздних стадиях и расширяет диапазон его допустимого количества на 25 %.
vent_drifter rare 2 Для всех видов непрерывное полное жизнеобеспечение постепенно повышает скорость размножения: за одни игровые сутки бонус нарастает до 7 %.
hive_sentinel rare 1 Только для этого вида снижает расход корма на каждую новую особь на 50 %, увеличивает начальную численность на 4 особи и увеличивает количество потомства за завершённые циклы размножения на 20 %.
hive_sentinel rare 2 Для всех видов добавляет 2 особи к начальной численности и повышает скорость размножения на 1 % за каждый другой прижившийся вид, до 12 %.
vault_crab rare 1 Только для этого вида снижает расход корма на каждую новую особь на 60 %.
vault_crab rare 2 Для всех видов снижает расход корма на каждую новую особь на 15 %.
crustal_echo rare 1 Только для этого вида: при основании колонии добавляет 6 особей и увеличивает скорость размножения на 3 % за каждую завершённую стадию жизни, максимум на 12 %.
crustal_echo rare 2 Для всех видов: увеличивает количество потомства за завершённые циклы размножения на 5 %.
hollow_choir rare 1 Только для этого вида повышает скорость размножения на 30 %.
hollow_choir rare 2 Для всех видов повышает скорость размножения на 10 %.

magmatic_annelid
salt_tortoise
veil_mantle
glasswing_mantis


+10 скорости
hollow_choir
ferric_sea_lily

hive_sentinel
mycelial_husk


-еда
vault_crab



Бонус 2 действует на все виды. Я ранжировал по реальной отдаче: безусловные эффекты выше условных, у которых цифра больше только при выполнении условия.

veil_mantle / glasswing_mantis / ferric_sea_lily / hollow_choir / spire_drake: +10 % к скорости размножения без условий. У всех пяти эффект одинаковый, редкость ничего не добавляет (у uncommon тот же бонус 2, что у legendary).
salt_tortoise: +15 % к скорости при численности < 2 500. Это самая большая цифра, и на ранней стадии бонус даёт больше любого другого, но после 2 500 он отключается.
hive_sentinel: +1 % за каждый другой прижившийся вид (до 12 %) и +2 особи к начальной численности. Он обгоняет +10 %, только когда прижилось больше 10 других видов, а потолок в 12 % требует 12 других видов из 15 в списке. Зато +2 к старту работает сразу.
mycelial_husk: то же до +12 %, но без +2 к старту, то есть строго хуже hive_sentinel.

vault_crab: −15 % корма на каждую новую особь. Если рост упирается в корм, это ≈ +18 % новых особей на то же количество корма. Если упирается в скорость размножения, бонус слабее остальных.


Остальные слабее или слишком узкие:

tidal_cephalopod даёт +10 % только при численности > 10 000;
vent_drifter даёт до +7 %, bone_walker до +6 %;
magmatic_annelid и crustal_echo дают +5 % потомства;
mantle_strider и glacial_wyrm поднимают предел скорости при большой численности на 10 %, это полезно, только если вы в этот предел упираетесь.

devices_prod.py×
import storages
import ioslots

def oxygen(self):
    atmosphere = get_component("atmosphere")
    if self.tier() >= 3:
        print(self.water_in.connect('liquid_tank_11'))

    if self.tier() == 4:
        print(self.input.connect('lead_cask_5'))

    #get_component("oxygen_generator")
    #print(atmosphere.get_co2())
    while True:

        if self.tier() == 4 and self.input.count() < 2:
            ioslots.input_take(self, 'fuel_rod', 2)

        if self.waste() >= 50:
            self.dump_waste()
        self.set_intake(atmosphere.get_co2() / 10)

        sleep(10)


def oxygen_10(self):
    atmosphere = get_component("atmosphere")
    if self.tier() >= 3:
        print(self.water_in.connect('liquid_tank_11'))

    if self.tier() == 4:
        print(self.input.connect('lead_cask_6'))

    #get_component("oxygen_generator")
    #print(atmosphere.get_co2())
    while True:

        if self.tier() == 4 and self.input.count() < 2:
            ioslots.input_take(self, 'fuel_rod', 2)

        if self.waste() >= 50:
            self.dump_waste()
        self.set_intake(atmosphere.get_co2() / 10)

        sleep(10)


def pressure(self):
    if self.tier() >= 3:
        print(self.water_in.connect('bulk_liquid_reservoir_1'))

    if self.tier() == 4:
        print(self.input.connect('lead_cask_5'))

    while True:

        if self.tier() == 4 and self.input.count() < 2:
            ioslots.input_take(self, 'fuel_rod', 2)

        #print(self.next_window_low(), self.gauge(), self.next_window_high())
        if self.next_window_low() < self.gauge() < self.next_window_high():
            self.sync()
            #print('+')
        #print(self.output(), self.effective_tier())


def pressure_10(self):
    if self.tier() >= 3:
        print(self.water_in.connect('bulk_liquid_reservoir_1'))

    if self.tier() == 4:
        print(self.input.connect('lead_cask_6'))

    while True:

        if self.tier() == 4 and self.input.count() < 2:
            ioslots.input_take(self, 'fuel_rod', 2)

        #print(self.next_window_low(), self.gauge(), self.next_window_high())
        if self.next_window_low() < self.gauge() < self.next_window_high():
            self.sync()
            #print('+')
        #print(self.output(), self.effective_tier())


def heater(self):
    profit = {}
    def set_pwr():
        for i in range(10):
            self.set_power(i)
            #print(self.thermal_state(), i, self.efficiency(), self.output())
            if self.efficiency() == 100:
                return i

    if self.tier() >= 3:
        print(self.steam_in.connect('gas_tank_2'))

    if self.tier() == 4:
        print(self.input.connect('lead_cask_5'))

    while True:

        if self.tier() == 4 and self.input.count() < 2:
            ioslots.input_take(self, 'fuel_rod', 2)

        state = self.thermal_state()
        #print(state)
        if state not in profit:
            good_n = set_pwr()
            profit[state] = good_n
        else:
            self.set_power(profit[state])
        sleep(10)

#self.thermal_state()
#self.efficiency()
#self.set_power()

def heater_10(self):
    profit = {}
    def set_pwr():
        for i in range(10):
            self.set_power(i)
            #print(self.thermal_state(), i, self.efficiency(), self.output())
            if self.efficiency() == 100:
                return i

    if self.tier() >= 3:
        print(self.steam_in.connect('gas_tank_2'))

    if self.tier() == 4:
        print(self.input.connect('lead_cask_6'))

    while True:

        if self.tier() == 4 and self.input.count() < 2:
            ioslots.input_take(self, 'fuel_rod', 2)

        state = self.thermal_state()
        #print(state)
        if state not in profit:
            good_n = set_pwr()
            profit[state] = good_n
        else:
            self.set_power(profit[state])
        sleep(10)



def turbine(self):
    print(self.steam_in.connect('gas_tank_2'))

    power = get_component("power_control")

    CHARGE_MAX = 0.9   # выше этого — генератор выключен
    CHARGE_MIN = 0.5   # ниже этого — генератор на максимуме

    def get_generator_power(charge: float) -> float:
        if charge <= CHARGE_MIN:
            return 1.0
        if charge >= CHARGE_MAX:
            return 0.0

        t = (charge - CHARGE_MIN) / (CHARGE_MAX - CHARGE_MIN)
        return 1.0 - t

    while True:
        power_total = power.total()
        delta = power_total.stored / power_total.capacity
        self.set_throttle(get_generator_power(delta))
        sleep(1)


def oil_generator(self):
    power = get_component("power_control")
    print(self.oil_in.connect(storages.storages['oil']))

    CHARGE_MAX = 0.7   # выше этого — генератор выключен
    CHARGE_MIN = 0.3   # ниже этого — генератор на максимуме

    def get_generator_power(charge: float) -> float:
        if charge <= CHARGE_MIN:
            return 1.0
        if charge >= CHARGE_MAX:
            return 0.0

        t = (charge - CHARGE_MIN) / (CHARGE_MAX - CHARGE_MIN)
        return 1.0 - t

    while True:
        power_total = power.total()
        delta = power_total.stored / power_total.capacity
        get_power = get_generator_power(delta)
        self.set_throttle(get_power)
        self.set_status(f'{get_power}')
        sleep(1)



def sol_panel(self):
    while True:
        clock = get_component("clock")
        #clock.get_elevation()
        #print(clock.get_elevation())
        self.set_tilt(90 - clock.get_elevation())



def thermal_cap(self):
    print(self.steam_out.connect('gas_tank_2'))
    print(self.set_throttle(1))

    while True:
        pressure = self.pressure()
        if pressure > 0.9:
            self.set_relief(1)
        if pressure < 0.5:
            self.set_relief(0)
        sleep(1)


def water_pump(self):
    print(self.water_out.connect('bulk_liquid_reservoir_1'))
    while True:
        print(self.set_throttle(1))
        sleep(20)



def oil_pump(self):
    print(self.oil_out.connect('bulk_liquid_reservoir_2'))
    liquid_tank_2 = get_component('bulk_liquid_reservoir_2')
    while True:
        if self.well_active():
            delta = (liquid_tank_2.fill_pct() - 1) * -1
            print(self.set_throttle(delta))
            sleep(2)
        else:
            print(self.set_throttle(0))
            sleep(2)


def drone_station_lifes(self, storages_biom):
    while True:
        if self.output.count() != 0:
            stacks = self.output.stacks()[0]
            print(stacks)
            stor_id = storages_biom[stacks.id]
            print(stor_id)
            print(self.output.connect(stor_id))
            ioslots.output_send(self, stacks.id, stacks.count)
            sleep(10)
        else:
            sleep(10)


def essence_liquifier(self, storages_biom):
    while True:

        if self.is_stalled() and self.stall_reason() == 'no_input':

            items_stor_count = {}
            for item_stor in storages_biom.keys():
                warehouse_id = get_component(storages_biom[item_stor])
                items_stor_count[item_stor] = warehouse_id.count(item_stor)
            print(items_stor_count)
            item_max_count = max(items_stor_count, key=items_stor_count.get)
            print(item_max_count)

            print(self.input.connect(storages_biom[item_max_count]))
            ioslots.input_take(self, item_max_count, 50)

            #random_item = random.choice(warehouse_stacks.keys())
        else:
            sleep(10)

def supply_dock(self, COMPANY, fabricator_id):

    comms = get_component("comms")
    fabricator_comp = get_component(fabricator_id)

    def comms_send(channel, msg):
        sent = comms.send(channel, msg)
        print(sent)
        if sent.status != "ok":
            print(sent.message)

    def set_company_order():
        orders = get_component("orders").list_orders()
        #print(orders.list_orders())
        for order in orders:
            if order.contractor_name == COMPANY:
                print(self.set_order(order.id))
        if not self.current_order():
            raise Exception('no orders !!!')

    def fabricator_request(current_order):
        #заказ в фабрикатор
        for req_item, req_count in current_order.requires.items():
            #сколько заказать
            _recipe = f'craft_{req_item}'
            if fabricator_comp.find_recipe(_recipe):
                req2fab_count = req_count - current_order.shipped.get(req_item, 0)
                print(f'COMM send job to: {fabricator_comp.name}. {_recipe} : {req2fab_count} ({req_count} - {current_order.shipped.get(req_item, 0)})')
                comms_send(f'jobs.{fabricator_comp.name}', {'recipe': _recipe, 'count': req2fab_count})

    current_order = self.current_order()
    #print(current_order)

    if not current_order:
        set_company_order()
        current_order = self.current_order()
        #print(current_order)

    #заказать что не хватает
    fabricator_request(current_order)

    #отправлять
    while self.current_order():
        for req_item, req_count in current_order.requires.items():
            #if name not in storages:
                #print(self.input.connect('inventory'))
            #else:
                #print(self.input.connect(storages[name]))

            print(self.input.connect(storages.get_storages(req_item)))
            ioslots.input_take(self, req_item, req_count - current_order.shipped.get(req_item, 0))

            self.set_enabled(True)

        sleep(5)

Дозатор
Засаливает четыре соседние ячейки поля по вертикали и горизонтали

Лампа для растений
Пока лампа включена и получает питание, она освещает четыре соседние клетки поля

Разбрызгиватель
Поливает четыре соседние по сторонам клетки поля (прямо сверху, снизу, слева и справа), пока у него есть питание и вода и он включён.

import storages
import ioslots



def worker(self, count):

    print('WORKER')
    print('LOAD')
    for req, n in self.get_recipe_inputs().items():
        print(self.input.connect(storages.get_storages(req)))
        #print('take', req, n, '*', count)
        #print(self.input.take(req, n * count))
        ioslots.input_take(self, req, n * count)

    print('')
    print('RUNNING')
    while self.is_running():
        print('is_running sleep ...')
        sleep(3)

    print('')
    print('OUTPUT')
    for mat in self.output.stacks():
        print(self.output.connect(storages.get_storages(mat.id)))
        #print('send', mat.id, mat.count)
        #print(self.output.send(mat.id, mat.count))
        ioslots.output_send(self, mat.id, mat.count)

    byproduct_stacks = self.byproduct.stacks()
    if byproduct_stacks:
        print('')
        print('BYPRODUCT')
        for mat in self.byproduct.stacks():
            print(self.byproduct.connect(storages.get_storages(mat.id)))
            #print('send', mat.id, mat.count)
            #print(self.output.send(mat.id, mat.count))
            ioslots.byproduct_send(self, mat.id, mat.count)

    print('')
    print('END')


def fabricator_main_order(self, need=None):
    print('fabricator_main_order', need)

    print(self.steam_in.connect(storages.get_storages('steam')))
    print(self.water_in.connect(storages.get_storages('water')))
    print(self.oil_in.connect(storages.get_storages('oil')))

    print(self.byproduct.connect(storages.get_storages('tar')))

    #max_capacity = self.output.capacity()
    max_capacity = 17
    # пересчитать алгаритмами , верхняя планка загрузки, нехватало места под игры

    ioslots.clear_input(self)
    ioslots.clear_output(self)
    ioslots.clear_byproduct(self)

    if need:
        while need > 0:
            batch = min(need, max_capacity)
            print('worker:', batch)
            worker(self, batch)
            need -= batch

    ioslots.clear_input(self)
    ioslots.clear_output(self)
    ioslots.clear_byproduct(self)


def smart_fabric(self, order_recipe_name, order_count, take_available_ingredients=False):
    print()

    self.set_status(f'{order_recipe_name}: {order_count}')

    item_catalog = get_component("item_catalog")

    debug = True

    # брать со склада уже готовые / или не брать  ????

    #build_order_queue = []
    #build_order_dict = {}

    build_order_list = []
    ingredients = {}

    #ingredients
    #components
    #parts

    #build_order_block_n

    def item_analiz(item_name, count, multiplier):

        if debug: print('item_analiz:', item_name, count, multiplier)

        item_obj = item_catalog.lookup(item_name)

        #проверка готовых на складах
        warehouse = get_component(storages.get_storages(item_name))
        if (item_obj.production_tier is None) or (item_obj.production_tier == 0):
            print('    не фабрикаторное, скип')
            pass
        else:
            count_delta = warehouse.count(item_name) - (count * multiplier)
            print('  count_delta: ', warehouse.count(item_name), '-', count , '*', multiplier, '=', count_delta)

            if count_delta < 0:
                count = count_delta * -1
                print('  count', count)
            else:
                print('  на скалде хватает')
                return


        #print(item_obj)
        #if item.category=='crafted' and production_tier>


        if (item_obj.production_tier is None) or (item_obj.production_tier == 0):
            ingredients.setdefault(item_obj.id, 0)
            ingredients[item_obj.id] += multiplier * count
            if debug: print('    ingredients add:', item_obj.id, multiplier * count)
        else:


            #если нету, перебрать весь список рецептов по
            #print(self.find_recipe('craft_smelter_pack_mk2'))
            #output_item='smelter_upgrade_pack_mk2'

            _problem_dict = {
                'smelter_upgrade_pack_mk2': 'craft_smelter_pack_mk2',
                'fabricator_upgrade_pack_mk2': 'craft_fabricator_pack_mk2',
                'smelter_upgrade_pack_mk3': 'craft_smelter_pack_mk3',
                'fabricator_upgrade_pack_mk3': 'craft_fabricator_pack_mk3',
                'oil_pump_upgrade_pack_mk2': 'craft_oil_pump_pack_mk2',
            }

            if item_name in _problem_dict:
                recipe = self.find_recipe(_problem_dict[item_name])
                build_order_list.insert(0, [_problem_dict[item_name], count])
            else:
                recipe = self.find_recipe(f'craft_{item_name}')
                build_order_list.insert(0, [f'craft_{item_obj.id}', count])

            for item_recipe_inputs in recipe.inputs:
                multiplier = recipe.inputs[item_recipe_inputs]
                #print(f' to item_analiz >', item_recipe_inputs)
                item_analiz(item_recipe_inputs, count, multiplier)


        #craft_circuit_panel {'iron_ingot': 1, 'glass': 2}
        #craft_control_unit {'circuit_panel': 1, 'titanium_ingot': 1, 'glass': 1}
        #craft_drone_small {'rare_earth_core': 1, 'titanium_ingot': 1, 'control_unit': 1}


    order_item_name = self.find_recipe(order_recipe_name).output_item

    item_analiz(order_item_name, order_count, 1)


    if debug: print()
    if debug: print(f'{build_order_list=}')
    if debug: print(f'{ingredients=}')

    for craft_recipe in build_order_list:

        # !!! что скипать при крафте !!!
        if craft_recipe[0] in ['craft_tar']:
            continue

        print(self.set_recipe(craft_recipe[0]))
        fabricator_main_order(self, need=craft_recipe[1])

    self.clear_recipe()
    self.clear_status()


def smart_comms(self):

    comms = get_component("comms")

    ioslots.clear_input(self)
    ioslots.clear_output(self)
    self.clear_status()
    self.clear_recipe()

    while True:

        received = comms.wait(f"jobs.{self.name}")
        if received.status != "ok":
            print(received)
            print('COMM ERR', received.message)
            break
        job = received.packet
        #print(job)
        print()
        print('----------------')
        print("COMM in Received", job.id, job.value)

        #{'recipe': radio_group, 'count': int(text_field)}

        print(self.set_recipe(job.value.recipe))
        #fabricator.fabricator_main_order(self, need=job.value.count)
        smart_fabric(self, job.value.recipe, job.value.count)

def smart_fabric(self, order_recipe_name, order_count, take_available_ingredients=False):

    self.set_status(f'{order_recipe_name}: {order_count}')

    item_catalog = get_component("item_catalog")

    debug = True

    # брать со склада уже готовые / или не брать  ????

    #build_order_queue = []
    #build_order_dict = {}

    build_order_list = []
    ingredients = {}

    #ingredients
    #components
    #parts

    #build_order_block_n

    def item_analiz(item_name, count, multiplier):

        if debug: print('item_analiz:', item_name, count, multiplier)


        item_obj = item_catalog.lookup(item_name)
        #print(item_obj)

        #if item.category=='crafted' and production_tier>


        if (item_obj.production_tier is None) or (item_obj.production_tier == 0):
            ingredients.setdefault(item_obj.id, 0)
            ingredients[item_obj.id] += multiplier * count
            if debug: print(' ingredients add:', item_obj.id, multiplier * count)
        else:
            _problem_dict = {
                'smelter_upgrade_pack_mk2': 'craft_smelter_pack_mk2',
                'fabricator_upgrade_pack_mk2': 'craft_fabricator_pack_mk2',
                'smelter_upgrade_pack_mk3': 'craft_smelter_pack_mk3',
                'fabricator_upgrade_pack_mk3': 'craft_fabricator_pack_mk3',

                'oil_pump_upgrade_pack_mk2': 'craft_oil_pump_pack_mk2',
            }

            if item_name in _problem_dict:
                recipe = self.find_recipe(_problem_dict[item_name])
                build_order_list.insert(0, [_problem_dict[item_name], multiplier * count])
            else:
                recipe = self.find_recipe(f'craft_{item_name}')
                build_order_list.insert(0, [f'craft_{item_obj.id}', multiplier * count])

            for item_recipe_inputs in recipe.inputs:
                multiplier = recipe.inputs[item_recipe_inputs]
                #print(f' to item_analiz >', item_recipe_inputs)
                item_analiz(item_recipe_inputs, count, multiplier)


        #craft_circuit_panel {'iron_ingot': 1, 'glass': 2}
        #craft_control_unit {'circuit_panel': 1, 'titanium_ingot': 1, 'glass': 1}
        #craft_drone_small {'rare_earth_core': 1, 'titanium_ingot': 1, 'control_unit': 1}


    order_item_name = self.find_recipe(order_recipe_name).output_item

    item_analiz(order_item_name, order_count, 1)


    if debug: print()
    if debug: print(f'{build_order_list=}')
    if debug: print(f'{ingredients=}')

    for craft_recipe in build_order_list:


        # !!! что скипать при крафте !!!
        if craft_recipe[0] in ['craft_tar']:
            continue

        print(self.set_recipe(craft_recipe[0]))
        #fabricator_main_order(self, need=craft_recipe[1])


    self.clear_recipe()
    self.clear_status()

0.75 — промышленный,  3 твердость,  20 Вт, затраты 1 бурение 3.76 wh

чем меньше, тем быстрее).

def move(self, coord_x, coord_y):
    print(self.name, 'goto ->', [coord_x, coord_y])
    self.nav.set_target(coord_x, coord_y)
    self.nav.set_throttle(1)

    while self.nav.get_distance_to(coord_x, coord_y) > 0.1:
        pass

    self.nav.brake()

def scan_survey(self):

    scan = self.sonar.scan()
    print(scan)
    print()

    print('scan.sites:', len(scan.sites))
    for site in scan.sites:
        print(site)

        #if not site.surveyed:
        survey = self.sonar.survey(site)
        print(survey)
        #print('no scan.sites, survey skip')

    print()
    print('scan.blocked:', len(scan.blocked))
    for block in scan.blocked:
        print(block)


def load(self):
    #print(self.output.connect('storage_bin_1'))
    #print(self.cargo.stacks())
    for stack in self.cargo.stacks():
        self.input.send(stack.id, stack.count)


def unload(self):
    #print(self.output.connect('storage_bin_1'))
    #print(self.cargo.stacks())
    for stack in self.cargo.stacks():
        ioslots.output_send(self, stack.id, stack.count)
        #self.output.send(stack.id, stack.count)


def battery_charging(self):
    while self.battery.level() != 1:
        sleep(2)


def drill(self, n):
    for _ in range(n):
        self.drill.mine()


def drill_worker(self, element, coord_x, coord_y, drill_count):

    #element = 'iron_ore'

    storage_id = storages.get_storages(element)
    print(self.output.connect(storage_id))

    move(self, 0, 0)
    unload(self)
    battery_charging(self)

    storage_component = get_component(storage_id)

    while True:
        if storage_component.count(element) < 500:
            move(self, coord_x, coord_y)
            drill(self, drill_count)
            move(self, 0, 0)
            unload(self)
            battery_charging(self)
        else:
            #print('fill_percent sleep ...')
            sleep(10)


def drill_auto(self, only_ore=None):

    #ores_type_id = ['iron_ore', 'glass', 'titanium_ingot', 'cobalt_ingot', 'rare_earth_core']

    if only_ore == 'neutronium':
        ores_coord = {'neutronium': [750, -80]}
    else:
        ores_coord = {
            #'iron_ore': [30, 30],
            #'silicon': [-70, -150],
            'titanium': [290, 170],
            'cobalt': [-80, -290],
            'rare_earth': [100, 560],
        }

        if only_ore in ores_coord:
            ores_coord = {only_ore: ores_coord[only_ore]}

    max_count = 1000

    move(self, 0, 0)
    if self.output.count() != 0:
        print(self.output.connect(storages.get_storages(self.output.stacks()[0].id)))
        unload(self)
    battery_charging(self)

    while True:

        while True:
            print('')
            ore_id = random.choice(ores_coord.keys())
            print('go for', ore_id)
            storage_id = storages.get_storages(ore_id)
            storage_component = get_component(storage_id)

            if storage_component.count(ore_id) < max_count:
                break
            else:
                print('ore_id storage 1000', storage_id)
                sleep(5)

        print(self.output.connect(storage_id))

        move(self, ores_coord[ore_id][0], ores_coord[ore_id][1])

        battery_wh_to_ore = self.battery.capacity() - self.battery.wh()
        battery_wh_to_base = battery_wh_to_ore * 2
        print('battery_wh_to_ore', battery_wh_to_ore)
        print('battery_wh_to_base', battery_wh_to_base)

        while True:

            wh_check = self.battery.wh() < battery_wh_to_base
            cargo_check = (self.output.capacity() - self.output.count()) <= 5
            if wh_check or cargo_check:
                break
            else:
                self.drill.mine()

        move(self, 0, 0)
        unload(self)
        battery_charging(self)

        sleep(5)


def ore_delivery(self):
    ores_type_id = {
        'iron_ore': {'coord': [-100, -40], 'drill': 'mining_drill_1'},
        'silicon': {'coord': [-70, -150], 'drill': 'mining_drill_2'},
        'lead_ore': {'coord': [-510, 270], 'drill': 'mining_drill_ind_1'},
    }
    #['iron_ore', 'glass', 'titanium_ingot', 'cobalt_ingot', 'rare_earth_core']

    max_count = 1000

    move(self, 0, 0)
    if self.output.count() != 0:
        print(self.output.connect(storages.get_storages(self.output.stacks()[0].id)))
        unload(self)
    battery_charging(self)

    while True:

        while True:
            print('')
            ore_id = random.choice(ores_type_id.keys())
            print('go for', ore_id)

            storage_id = storages.get_storages(ore_id)
            storage_component = get_component(storage_id)


            if storage_component.count(ore_id) > max_count:
                # много в хранилище
                print('ore_id storage 1000', storage_id)
                break
            else:
                drill = get_component(ores_type_id[ore_id]['drill'])
                if drill.output.count() < 100:
                    # мало на буровой
                    break

                move(self, ores_type_id[ore_id]['coord'][0], ores_type_id[ore_id]['coord'][1])
                print(self.input.connect(ores_type_id[ore_id]['drill']))

                ioslots.input_take(self, ore_id, 10000)

                move(self, 0, 0)
                print(self.output.connect(storages.get_storages(self.output.stacks()[0].id)))
                unload(self)
                battery_charging(self)

        sleep(5)



def salt(self):

    water_pumps = [
        ['water_pump_4', [350, 180]],
        ['water_pump_1', [160, -330]],
        ['water_pump_3', [10, -440]],
        ['water_pump_2', [-30, -470]],
    ]

    while True:
        move(self, 0, 0)

        if self.output.count() != 0:
            print(self.output.connect(storages.get_storages(self.output.stacks()[0].id)))
            unload(self)

        battery_charging(self)

        storage_id = storages.get_storages('salt')
        storage_component = get_component(storage_id)

        while True:
            if storage_component.count('salt') > 500:
                # много в хранилище
                print('ore_id storage 500, sleep ...', storage_id)
                sleep(20)
            else:
                break

        print('go for solt')
        for pump in water_pumps:
            pump_id = pump[0]
            coord = pump[1]
            move(self, coord[0], coord[1])
            pump_comp = get_component(pump_id)
            self.input.connect(pump_id)
            while pump_comp.output.count() != 20:
                sleep(5)

            ioslots.input_take(self, 'salt', 20)

        sleep(5)

import storages
import ioslots
import random

harvester_1 = get_component('harvester_1')


def cell_around_4(coord):
    greed_n = [n for n in range(1, 25)]
    greed_l = ['A', 'B','C','D','E','F','G','H']

    _cell_around = []

    coord_letter = coord[:1]
    coord_number = int(coord[1:])


    _new_letter_index = greed_l.index(coord_letter) - 1
    #print(_new_letter_index)
    if 0 <= _new_letter_index <= 7:
        _cell_around.append(f'{greed_l[_new_letter_index]}{coord_number}')

    _new_letter_index = greed_l.index(coord_letter) + 1
    #print(_new_letter_index)
    if 0 <= _new_letter_index <= 7:
        _cell_around.append(f'{greed_l[_new_letter_index]}{coord_number}')


    _new_number = coord_number - 1
    #print(_new_number)
    if 1 <= _new_number <= 24:
        _cell_around.append(f'{coord_letter}{_new_number}')

    _new_number = coord_number + 1
    #print(_new_number)
    if 1 <= _new_number <= 24:
        _cell_around.append(f'{coord_letter}{_new_number}')

    return _cell_around

def grow_lamp(self):
    '''
    <Cell: id='C15', plant=None, growth=0, status='empty', lit=False, watered=False, salted=False, manual_light_remaining=0, manual_water_remaining=0, manual_salt_remaining=0, fertilized=False, fertilizer_remaining=0, ...>
    <Cell: id='D15', plant='grandbloom', growth=0, status='stalled', lit=False, watered=False, salted=False, manual_light_remaining=0, manual_water_remaining=0, manual_salt_remaining=0, fertilized=False, fertilizer_remaining=0, ...>

    '''
    while True:

        turn_off = True
        for pos in cell_around_4(self.position()):
            cell = harvester_1.cell(pos)
            if cell.plant and cell.status != 'mature':
                print(self.position(), cell)
                self.set_enabled(True)
                turn_off = False
                break
        if turn_off:
            self.set_enabled(False)

        sleep(2)

        #print(self.position())
        #print(self.status() )
        #print(self.is_active())
        #print(self.is_supplied())

def crop_automator(self, sectors):

    self.input.connect('large_warehouse_5')
    #seeds_warehouse = get_component('large_warehouse_5')

    #self.plant(sector: str, seed_id: str)
    #self.apply(sector: str, item_id: str)
    #self.harvest(sector: str)

    print(self.clear_queue())

    while self.result_count() != 0:
        print(self.next_result())


    def plant(self, sector, seed):
        ioslots.input_take(self, seed, 1)
        print(self.plant(sector, seed))

    def fertilize(self, sector):
        pass

    def harvest(self, sector):
        cell_info = self.cell(sector)
        res = self.harvest(sector)
        print('harvest:', cell_info.plant, ', forage:', cell_info.forage, ', status:', res.status)


    large_warehouse_2 = get_component('large_warehouse_2')

    while True:

        if large_warehouse_2.count('forage') < 500:
            self.output.connect('large_warehouse_2')
            ioslots.output_send(self, 'forage', 200)

        while self.output.count() > 45000:
            print('self.output.count', self.output.count())
            sleep(20)

        #while self.queue_count() > 30:
            #print('queue_count', self.queue_count())
            #sleep(5)


        for sector in sectors:
            #print(sector)
            cell = self.cell(sector)
            #print(cell)
            match cell.status:
                case "empty":
                    print('сажать')
                    plant(self, sector, sectors[sector])

                #case "growing":
                    #print('удобрять')
                    #harvest(self, sector)

                case "mature":
                    print('убирать')
                    harvest(self, sector)

            while self.current_job():
                sleep(2)

            while self.result_count() != 0:
                print(self.next_result())


def plant_terraformer(self):

    print(self.water_in.connect((storages.storages['water'])))
    print(self.set_enabled(True))

    while True:

        #брать с углового если он не пустой, иначе рендом из остальных
        automator_comp = get_component('crop_automator_5')
        if automator_comp.output.count() != 0:
            print('crop_automator_5')
            automator_id = 'crop_automator_5'
        else:
            #список автоматоров
            print('список автоматоров')
            crop_automators = []
            for machine in get_component('outpost_home').harvesting_machines():
                if machine.type_id == 'crop_automator':
                    #get_component(machine.id)
                    crop_automators.append(machine.id)
            print(crop_automators)

            # выбрать автоматор
            while True:
                print('выбрать автоматор')
                print(crop_automators)

                if not crop_automators:
                    automator_id = None
                    break

                automator_id = random.choice(crop_automators)
                automator_comp = get_component(automator_id)
                _count = automator_comp.output.count()
                if _count > 0:
                    print(automator_id, _count, 'качаем с него')
                    break
                else:
                    print(automator_id, _count, 'remove')
                    crop_automators.remove(automator_id)

        #качать с автоматора
        print('качать с автоматора')

        if automator_id:

            while True:

                if automator_comp.output.count() == 0:
                    print(automator_id, 'пустой')
                    break

                input_stacks = ioslots.stacks_to_dict(self.input.stacks())
                print(input_stacks, self.batch_size())

                finput_stacks_forage = input_stacks.get('forage', 0)
                if finput_stacks_forage < 6600:
                    print(self.input.connect(automator_id))
                    ioslots.input_take(self, 'forage', 6600 - finput_stacks_forage)

                finput_stacks_salt = input_stacks.get('salt', 0)
                if finput_stacks_salt != 14:
                    print(self.input.connect(storages.storages['salt']))
                    ioslots.input_take(self, 'salt', 14 - finput_stacks_salt)

                #fertilizer       10
                #fertilizer_mk2   30
                #fertilizer_mk3   50
                finput_stacks_fertilizer = input_stacks.get('fertilizer_mk2', 0)
                if finput_stacks_fertilizer < 2:
                    print(self.input.connect(storages.storages['fertilizer_mk2']))
                    ioslots.input_take(self, 'fertilizer_mk2', 10)


                finput_stacks_growth_accelerant = input_stacks.get('growth_accelerant', 0)
                if finput_stacks_growth_accelerant < 1:
                    print(self.input.connect(storages.storages['growth_accelerant']))
                    ioslots.input_take(self, 'growth_accelerant', 1)


                sleep(10)

        sleep(10)

def thermostat():
    required_range_t = self.required_range()
    #[250, 320]
    hard_range_t = [required_range_t[0]+10, required_range_t[1]-10]
    middle_range_t = required_range_t[0] + ((required_range_t[1] - required_range_t[0]) / 2)

    print(hard_range_t)
    print(middle_range_t)

    good_temp = False

    while not good_temp:
        _tp = self.temperature()

        delta = middle_range_t - _tp


        if -10 < delta < 10:
            print('good_temp')
            good_temp = True
        elif delta < -100:
            self.set_heat(0)
            self.set_cool(100)
        elif delta > 100:
            self.set_heat(100)
            self.set_cool(0)
        elif delta < -50:
            self.set_heat(0)
            self.set_cool(50)
        elif delta > 50:
            self.set_heat(50)
            self.set_cool(0)
        elif delta < -20:
            self.set_heat(0)
            self.set_cool(20)
        elif delta > 20:
            self.set_heat(20)
            self.set_cool(0)
        #good_temp = True

    print('set netral tp')
    self.set_cool(1)
    self.set_heat(1)


def work():

    self.set_status('output')
    while self.output.count() != 0:
        sleep(1)


    self.set_status('input')
    bio_lab_5 = get_component('bio_lab_5')
    print(self.input.connect('bio_lab_5'))


    while True:
        if bio_lab_5.output.stacks():
            ioslots.input_take(self, bio_lab_5.output.stacks()[0].id, 1)
            break
        else:
            sleep(1)


    self.set_status('work')
    #print(self.input.stacks())
    item_name = self.input.stacks()[0].id
    print(item_name)
    print(self.set_recipe(item_name))
    print(self.recipe())
    print(self.load(self.recipe()))
    print(self.fragment())

    print(self.input.connect('warehouse_7'))
    for req_mat, req_mat_count in self.required_materials().items():
        #print(req_mat, req_mat_count)

        storag = volcanic.get_storages(req_mat)
        print(self.input.connect(storag))

        while True:
            warehouse_comp = get_component(storag)
            if warehouse_comp.count(req_mat) < req_mat_count:
                print(self.id, '!!! need:', req_mat)
                self.set_status(f'!!! need: {req_mat}')
                sleep(10)
            else:
                self.set_status('work')
                break

        ioslots.input_take(self, req_mat, req_mat_count)

    thermostat()
    print(self.cast())



def main():

    print(self.steam_in.connect('gas_tank_2'))
    print(self.water_in.connect('liquid_tank_11'))

    while True:
        work()

main()

def make_recipe_list():

    recipe_list = {}

    while True:

        print(self.take_from(collector))
        analyze = self.analyze()
        print(analyze)
        #fragment = {analyze.info.fragment_id: analyze.info.required_recipe}
        #print(fragment)
        if analyze.info.fragment_id not in recipe_list:
            recipe_list.update({analyze.info.fragment_id: analyze.info.required_recipe})
        print(self.discard())
        print(len(recipe_list), 'recipe_list', recipe_list)
        sleep(5)

def list_full_zone_req_counter():

    bio_exchange_5 = get_component("bio_exchange_5")

    orders = bio_exchange_5.orders()

    #active_order = self.active_order()

    print(len(orders))

    #bioms: 'frozen' 'coastal' 'geothermal' 'volcanic' 'deep'

    counter = 1

    #shopping_list = {'cryo_solvent': 0, 'protein_marker': 0}
    shopping_list = {'protein_marker': 0, 'chelating_agent': 0}


    #  !!!!!!!!! поменять словарь и биом


    for n, order in enumerate(orders):
        #print(order.biome)
        #biome='coastal'
        #if order.biome=='coastal' and order.status=='available':
        if order.biome=='volcanic':
            #print(counter , order)
            print('=================')
            print(counter , order.id)

            for order_req in order.requires:

                print(order_req, order.requires[order_req])
                print(recipes[order_req])

                for ned_buy_req in recipes[order_req]:
                    _plus = recipes[order_req][ned_buy_req] * order.requires[order_req]
                    shopping_list[ned_buy_req] += _plus

            print()
            print('geothermal', shopping_list)

            counter += 1

def list_full_req_for_deep_biocaster():

    bio_exchange = get_component("bio_exchange_7")

    orders = bio_exchange.orders()

    #active_order = self.active_order()

    print(len(orders))

    #bioms: 'frozen' 'coastal' 'geothermal' 'volcanic' 'deep'

    counter = 1

    #shopping_list = {'cryo_solvent': 0, 'protein_marker': 0}
    #shopping_list = {'protein_marker': 0, 'chelating_agent': 0}

    shopping_list = {}

    for n, order in enumerate(orders):
        #print(order.biome)
        #biome='coastal'
        #if order.biome=='coastal' and order.status=='available':

        if order.biome=='deep' and order.status != 'complete':
            #print(counter , order)
            print('=================')
            print(counter , order.id)

            for order_req_item in order.requires:
                order_req_item_count = order.requires[order_req_item]
                print(order_req_item, order_req_item_count)
                for need_to_trans_item in bio_caster_recipes[order_req_item]:
                    print(' ', need_to_trans_item, order_req_item_count * bio_caster_recipes[order_req_item][need_to_trans_item])

                    shopping_list.setdefault(need_to_trans_item, 0)
                    shopping_list[need_to_trans_item] += order_req_item_count * bio_caster_recipes[order_req_item][need_to_trans_item]

                    print('  ',shopping_list, sum(shopping_list[item] for item in shopping_list))
                #print(recipes[order_req])

                #for ned_buy_req in recipes[order_req]:
                    #_plus = recipes[order_req][ned_buy_req] * order.requires[order_req]
                    #shopping_list[ned_buy_req] += _plus
            counter += 1

    print()
    print('list_full_req_for_deep_biocaster', shopping_list)

def list_full_req_for_volcanic_biocaster():

    bio_exchange_5 = get_component("bio_exchange_5")

    orders = bio_exchange_5.orders()

    #active_order = self.active_order()

    print(len(orders))

    #bioms: 'frozen' 'coastal' 'geothermal' 'volcanic' 'deep'

    counter = 1

    #shopping_list = {'cryo_solvent': 0, 'protein_marker': 0}
    #shopping_list = {'protein_marker': 0, 'chelating_agent': 0}

    shopping_list = {}

    for n, order in enumerate(orders):
        #print(order.biome)
        #biome='coastal'
        #if order.biome=='coastal' and order.status=='available':
        if order.biome=='volcanic':
            #print(counter , order)
            print('=================')
            print(counter , order.id)

            for order_req_item in order.requires:
                order_req_item_count = order.requires[order_req_item]
                print(order_req_item, order_req_item_count)
                for need_to_trans_item in bio_caster_recipes[order_req_item]:
                    print(' ', need_to_trans_item, order_req_item_count * bio_caster_recipes[order_req_item][need_to_trans_item])

                    shopping_list.setdefault(need_to_trans_item, 0)
                    shopping_list[need_to_trans_item] += order_req_item_count * bio_caster_recipes[order_req_item][need_to_trans_item]

                    print(shopping_list)
                #print(recipes[order_req])

                #for ned_buy_req in recipes[order_req]:
                    #_plus = recipes[order_req][ned_buy_req] * order.requires[order_req]
                    #shopping_list[ned_buy_req] += _plus
            counter += 1

    print()
    print('list_full_req_for_volcanic_biocaster', shopping_list)

fabricator.py×
import storages
import ioslots



def clear_io(self):
    if self.input.count() > 0: ioslots.clear_input(self)
    if self.output.count() > 0: ioslots.clear_output(self)
    if self.byproduct.count() > 0: ioslots.clear_byproduct(self)


def fab_init(self):

    print(self.steam_in.connect(storages.get_storages('steam')))
    print(self.water_in.connect(storages.get_storages('water')))
    print(self.oil_in.connect(storages.get_storages('oil')))
    print(self.byproduct.connect(storages.get_storages('tar')))

    #clear_io(self)


def worker(self, count):
    print('---[ worker:', count, ']------')
    print('')

    print('load')
    for req, n in self.get_recipe_inputs().items():
        print(self.input.connect(storages.get_storages(req)))
        #print('take', req, n, '*', count)
        #print(self.input.take(req, n * count))
        ioslots.input_take(self, req, n * count)


    while True:
        print('')
        print('running ...')
        while self.is_running():
            sleep(1)

        print('')
        print('output')
        for mat in self.output.stacks():
            print(self.output.connect(storages.get_storages(mat.id)))
            #print('send', mat.id, mat.count)
            #print(self.output.send(mat.id, mat.count))
            ioslots.output_send(self, mat.id, mat.count)

        byproduct_stacks = self.byproduct.stacks()
        if byproduct_stacks:
            print('')
            print('byproduct')
            for mat in self.byproduct.stacks():
                print(self.byproduct.connect(storages.get_storages(mat.id)))
                #print('send', mat.id, mat.count)
                #print(self.output.send(mat.id, mat.count))
                ioslots.byproduct_send(self, mat.id, mat.count)

        if self.is_running():
            print()
            print('дожаривание ...')
        else:
            break

    print('')
    print('worker end')
    print('')




def make_build_order(self, order_recipe_name, order_count):
    '''
    comm вход
     разбивка на батчи по 15
     анализатор
     проверка необходимого на складе???
     воркер
    '''

    #item_catalog = get_component("item_catalog")
    debug_print = True
    build_order = {}
    #ingredients = {}

    print('---[ smart_fabric:', order_recipe_name, order_count, ']---')

    def item_analiz(recipe_id, recipe_count, recipe_multiplier):
        # рекурсивный перебор _итемов_ внутри реквайерсов из рецептов

        if debug_print:
            print()
            print('item_analiz:', recipe_id, recipe_count, recipe_multiplier)

        recipe_obj = self.find_recipe(recipe_id)
        if recipe_obj:
            # фабрикаторное
            #item_obj = item_catalog.lookup(recipe_id.output_item)

            #добавление в билд-ордер
            print('build_order update:', build_order.get(recipe_id, 0), recipe_count, recipe_multiplier)

            build_order[recipe_id] = build_order.get(recipe_id, 0) + (recipe_count * recipe_multiplier)

            print(f'{build_order=}')
            print(f'{recipe_obj.inputs=}')

            for req_item, req_count in recipe_obj.inputs.items():
                #inputs={'iron_ingot': 1, 'glass': 2},
                #recipe = self.find_recipe()

                item_analiz(f'craft_{req_item}', recipe_count * recipe_multiplier, req_count)

        else:
            # не абрикаторное, скип
            print('  не фабрикаторное, скип')


    # рекурсивное формирование билдордера
    item_analiz(order_recipe_name, order_count, 1)

    print()
    print(f'{build_order=}')

    return build_order


def smart_fabric(self, order_recipe_name, order_count):

    clear_io(self)

    print()
    print('===[ main_order:', order_recipe_name, order_count, ']===================================')
    print('')

    if not order_recipe_name:
        print('not order_recipe_name')
        return

    take_available_ingredients = True

    order_count_ready = 0

    # smelter_upgrade_pack_mk3: 15/200
    self.set_status(f'{order_recipe_name}: {order_count_ready}/{order_count}')

    #верхняя планка одной партии
    max_capacity = 10

    balance_count = order_count
    if balance_count:
        while balance_count > 0:
            batch_count = min(balance_count, max_capacity)

            #print()
            #print('------------------------')
            #print('batch_count:', batch_count)

            build_order = make_build_order(self, order_recipe_name, batch_count)
            #print(build_order)
            #build_order={'craft_control_unit': 1, 'craft_circuit_panel': 1}
            #build_order.keys()

            print()
            print('----------------------')
            print('формирование запроса в воркер:')
            print()

            #print(reversed(build_order.keys()))
            rev_build_order_keys = reversed(build_order.keys())
            # реверсивный запрос в воркера
            for recipe_name in rev_build_order_keys:
                print(recipe_name)

                worker_count = 0

                # что не крафтим
                if recipe_name in ['craft_tar']:
                    print('skip: ', recipe_name)
                    continue

                #основной
                if recipe_name == order_recipe_name:
                    worker_count = build_order[recipe_name]

                #полная проверка
                else:
                    recipe_obj = self.find_recipe(recipe_name)
                    recipe_item_id = recipe_obj.output_item
                    warehouse = get_component(storages.get_storages(recipe_item_id))

                    available_count = warehouse.count(recipe_item_id)
                    required_count = build_order[recipe_name]
                    worker_count = max(0, required_count - available_count)

                #уход в воркера
                if worker_count != 0:
                    res_set_recipe = self.set_recipe(recipe_name)
                    if res_set_recipe.status == 'ok':
                        print('set_recipe:', recipe_name)
                        print('worker send:', worker_count)
                        print('')
                        worker(self, worker_count)
                        sleep(2)
                    else:
                        warn('fabricator set_recipe_status err !!!', res_set_recipe)
                        return
                else:
                    print('на складе достаточно, воркер скип')

            #print('rev_build_order_keys[:-1] пустой, все скип')

            #сдвинуть счетчик в пачке
            order_count_ready += batch_count
            self.set_status(f'{order_recipe_name}: {order_count_ready}/{order_count}')
            balance_count -= batch_count

    self.clear_recipe()
    self.clear_status()


def smart_comms(self):

    comms = get_component("comms")

    self.clear_status()
    self.clear_recipe()

    fab_init(self)
    clear_io(self)

    self.clear_status()
    self.clear_recipe()

    while True:

        received = comms.wait(f"jobs.{self.name}")
        if received.status != "ok":
            print(received)
            print('COMM ERR', received.message)
            break
        job = received.packet
        #print(job)
        print()
        print('----------------')
        print("COMM in Received", job.id, job.value)

        #{'recipe': radio_group, 'count': int(text_field)}

        print(self.set_recipe(job.value.recipe))
        #fabricator.fabricator_main_order(self, need=job.value.count)
        smart_fabric(self, job.value.recipe, job.value.count)

directions = ["north", "south", "west", "east"]


def go():

    prev_step = road_good[-1]
    #print(prev_step)

    print(vault.position)

    #for dir in directions:
    while True:
        dir = random.choice(directions)


        if prev_step == "north" and dir == "south":
            continue
        elif prev_step == "south" and dir == "north":
            continue
        elif prev_step == "west" and dir == "east":
            continue
        elif prev_step == "east" and dir == "west":
            continue

        res = vault.move(dir)
        #print(dir, res.status)
        if res.status == "path":
            road_good.append(dir)
            break
            #return dir
        elif res.status == "wall":
            continue
        elif res.status == "exit":
            road_good.append(dir)
            print(road_good)

            escape = vault.escape()
            if escape.status == "ok":
              transmitter = get_component("transmitter")
              transmitter.connect("earth")
              transmitter.transmit(self.contract.id, escape.key)
            else:
              print(escape.message)


            raise Exception('exit')


def next_move():
    for step in road_good:
        vault.move(step)

    step_count = 1

    while True:

        go()
        print(len(road_good), road_good)
        #sleep(1)

        step_count += 1


next_move()

def road(move):
    res = vault.move(move)
    if move == "north": print(f"/|\\", res.status)
    elif move == "south": print(f"\\|/", res.status)
    elif move == "west": print(f"<-", res.status)
    elif move == "east": print(f"->", res.status)

import auto
import ioslots
import storages


blueprint = get_component("construction_blueprint")

#print(blueprint.pending_constructions())
#for bp in blueprint.pending_constructions():
    #print(bp.position.x)


def load_for_build():
    print('load_for_build')

    # словарь что нужно для стройки
    required_items = {}
    pending_constructions = blueprint.pending_constructions()
    for bp in pending_constructions:
        required_items[bp.required_item] = required_items.get(bp.required_item, 0) + bp.required_count
    #print(required_items)

    self_stacks_dict = ioslots.stacks_to_dict(self.cargo.stacks())
    #inv_stacks_dict = ioslots.stacks_to_dict(get_component('inventory').stacks())

    # всего ли хватает: карго + инвентарь базы - что нужно
    _not_enough_item = {}

    #print(required_items)

    for item in required_items:
        if not item: continue
        st = get_component(storages.get_storages(item))
        #print(item, st, st.count(item))

        _diff =  self_stacks_dict.get(item, 0) + st.count(item) - required_items[item]
        if _diff < 0:
            #print(item, required_items[item], self_stacks_dict.get(item, 0), inv_stacks_dict.get(item, 0), '_diff:', _diff)
            #print(item, _diff)
            _not_enough_item[item] = _diff

    if _not_enough_item:
        warn(self.name, '_not_enough_item:')
        for _item in _not_enough_item:
            warn(' ', _item, '-', _not_enough_item[_item] * -1)
        return False


    # выгрузить лишее фул что вообще не нужно
    for item in self_stacks_dict:
        if item not in required_items:
            #print(self.output.send(item, self_stacks_dict[item]))
            self.output.connect(storages.get_storages(item))
            ioslots.output_send(self, item, self_stacks_dict[item])

    # загрузка недостатвков, выгрузить лишнее.
    for item in required_items:
        count_self = self_stacks_dict.get(item, 0)
        #count_inv = inv_stacks_dict.get(item, 0)
        count_req = required_items[item]
        _diff = count_self - count_req
        if _diff == 0:
            continue
        elif _diff < 0:
            #print(self.input.take(item, _diff * -1))
            self.input.connect(storages.get_storages(item))
            ioslots.input_take(self, item, _diff * -1)
        elif _diff > 0:
            #print(self.output.send(item, _diff))
            #print(self, item, _diff[item])
            self.output.connect(storages.get_storages(item))
            ioslots.output_send(self, item, _diff)

    return True

#load_for_build()

def go_and_build(recharge_base):
    print('go_and_build')

    pending_constructions = blueprint.pending_constructions()

    while pending_constructions:

        if self.battery.level() < 0.2:
            auto.move(self, recharge_base[0], recharge_base[1])
            auto.battery_charging(self)

        _distance_to_bp = {}

        for bp in pending_constructions:
            #print(bp)
            #print(bp.position)
            #required_item='power_line_segment', required_count=1>

            #if bp.required_item == 'liquid_pipe_bridge':
                #continue

            if (bp.kind == 'deconstruct') or ioslots.have_inside(self.cargo.stacks(), bp.required_item):
                _distance = self.nav.get_distance_to(bp.position.x, bp.position.y)
                _distance_to_bp[_distance] = bp
            else:
                warn(self.name, 'dont have inside', bp.required_item)
                break

        #print(_distance_to_bp)
        #print(_distance_to_bp.keys())
        #print(sorted(_distance_to_bp.keys()))
        #print(_distance_to_bp[sorted(_distance_to_bp.keys())[0]])

        next_nearest_bp = _distance_to_bp[sorted(_distance_to_bp.keys())[0]]

        auto.move(self, next_nearest_bp.position.x, next_nearest_bp.position.y)
        print(self.constructor.execute(next_nearest_bp.id))

        # обновить для  while
        pending_constructions = blueprint.pending_constructions()

bio_conditioner_1.py×
import ioslots


def check_rule(fragment_report, active_rule_id):

    #{'glow': 'white', 'brightness': 3, 'smell': 'rotten', 'gunk': 63, 'cracks': 'small', 'feel': 'hard', 'twitch': 'still', 'bugs': 3, 'weight': 109, 'sound': 'thud'}

    '''
    === fragment_report ===
      glow : white
      brightness : 3
      smell : rotten
      gunk : 63
      cracks : small
      feel : hard
    > twitch : still
      bugs : 3
      weight : 109
      sound : thud

    '''

    print('--- fragment_report ------')
    for k,v in fragment_report.items():
        if k == active_rule_id:
            print('>', k, ':', v)
        else:
            print(' ', k, ':', v)

    match active_rule_id:
        case 'glow':
            return True if fragment_report[active_rule_id] in ['blue', 'green', 'purple'] else False

        case 'brightness':
            if fragment_report['glow'] in ['blue', 'green']:
                return True if (45 <= fragment_report[active_rule_id] <= 80) else False
            elif fragment_report['glow'] == 'purple':
                return True if (20 <= fragment_report[active_rule_id] <= 50) else False
            elif fragment_report['glow'] in ['white', 'dark']:
                return False
            return False

        case 'smell':
            return True if fragment_report[active_rule_id] in ['salty', 'fishy'] else False

        case 'gunk':
            return True if (70 <= fragment_report[active_rule_id] <= 85) else False

        case 'cracks':
            return True if fragment_report[active_rule_id] in ['none', 'small'] else False

        case 'feel':
            return True if fragment_report[active_rule_id] == 'hard' else False

        case 'twitch':
            return True if fragment_report[active_rule_id] in ['weak', 'still'] else False

        case 'bugs':
            return True if (1 <= fragment_report[active_rule_id] <= 3) else False

        case 'weight':
            if not fragment_report['gunk'] < 70:
                #return True if fragment_report[active_rule_id] <= 300 else False
                return True if (180 <= fragment_report[active_rule_id] <= 300) else False
            else:
                return True if (180 <= fragment_report[active_rule_id] <= 260) else False

        case 'sound':
            # тут возможно всю групу касается
            if fragment_report[active_rule_id] == 'ding':
                return True
            elif fragment_report[active_rule_id] == 'thud':
                return True if fragment_report['cracks'] in ['none', 'small'] else False

        case _:
            print('WRONG active_rule_id !!!')

#print(check_rule(self.report(), self.current()))



def examination():
    print('examination')

    fragment_report = self.report()

    while self.is_running():
        print('============================')
        print('stage:', self.stage())

        active_rule_id = self.current()

        res_check_rule = check_rule(fragment_report, active_rule_id)
        print('res_check_rule', res_check_rule)

        result = self.accept() if res_check_rule else self.reject()
        print(result.status, result)
        if result.status in ["ok", "conditioned"]:
            print('result.status', result.status)
        else:
            warn('result.status', result.status, result)

        #sleep(3)

    '''
    report = self.report()
    while self.is_running():
      property_id = self.current()
      result = self.accept() if passes_rule(property_id, report) else self.reject()
      if result.status not in ["ok", "conditioned"]:
        print(result.message)

    '''

#examination()



def work():

    self.set_status('output')
    while self.output.count() != 0:
        sleep(1)

    self.set_status('input')
    bio_lab_7 = get_component('bio_lab_7')
    print(self.input.connect('bio_lab_7'))
    while True:
        if bio_lab_7.output.stacks():
            ioslots.input_take(self, bio_lab_7.output.stacks()[0].id, 1)
            break
        else:
            sleep(1)

    print(self.load(self.input.stacks()[0].id))

    self.set_status('work')
    examination()



def main():
    while True:
        work()


main()

comms = get_component("comms")
component = get_component('fabricator_1')

recipes = component.list_recipes()



def comms_send(channel, msg):
    sent = comms.send(channel, msg)
    print(sent)
    if sent.status != "ok":
        print(sent.message)


recipes_list = {}

def print_oneline_recipes():
    for tier_n in range(0, 6):
        #print()
        #print('Tier', tier_n)
        for recipe in recipes:

            if recipe.tier == tier_n:
                rus_name = recipe.name[recipe.name.find('→')+2:].capitalize()
                #print(tier_n, rus_name, recipe.id)
                recipes_list[recipe.id] = {'rus_name': rus_name, 'tier_n': tier_n}
                #print(f'{_name:<30}  {recipe.id:<30} {recipe.inputs:<30} {recipe.fluid_inputs}')

print_oneline_recipes()


while True:
    panel.clear()

    icon_size = 12
    for n, recipe in enumerate(recipes_list):
        #print(recipe[6:])

        if n == 0:
            y = 7
        else:
            y = 7 + n * icon_size

        _text = f'tier{recipes_list[recipe]['tier_n']}  {recipes_list[recipe]['rus_name'][:20]}'
        #label(x: float, y: float, text: str, style?: str, color?: str) → None
        #panel.label(x=10, y=y, text=recipes_list[recipe]['rus_name'][:30], style="muted")
        #panel.draw_text(x: float, y: float, text: str, size?: float, color?: str, wrap?: float) → None
        panel.draw_text(x=10, y=y+7, text=_text, size=10, color="text-muted")

        panel.draw_icon(x=170, y=y, item_id=recipe[6:], size=icon_size)

    #radio_group(key: str, x: float, y: float, options: list[str], default?: str | int, row_height?: float) → str | None
    radio_group = panel.radio_group(
        key='smelter_2_recipe3',
        x=195, y=8,
        options=recipes_list.keys(),
        row_height=12
    )

    # ПРАВАЯ ПАНЕЛЬ

    panel.label(x=400, y=30, text=component.name, style="caption")

    #panel.text_field(key=text_field1, 300, 20, 30, default?: str, placeholder?: str, label?: str, size?: float) → str
    text_field = panel.text_field(
        key='text_field1',
        x=400, y=50, w=50,
        default='20',
        placeholder='n',
        #label='count',
        size=24
    )
    #panel.set_text(key: str, text: str) → None

        key='button1',
        x=400, y=80, w=50, h=30,
        label='Send'
    ):
        comms_send(f'jobs.{component.name}', {'recipe': radio_group, 'count': int(text_field)})


    #print(fabricator_1.get_recipe())
    #print(fabricator_1.is_running())
    #print(fabricator_1.get_progress())

    #component.is_running()
    #("running", "paused", "error", "idle")
    _status = "running" if component.is_running() else "idle"
    panel.status_dot(382, 28, 4, _status)

        420, 170, radius=30,

    )

    #значек активного рецепта
    #print(component.get_recipe()[6:])
