drones.py×
import random
import data

def goto(self, coord_x, coord_y, throttle=1):
    print(self.name, 'goto ->', [coord_x, coord_y])
    while True:
        res = self.go_to(coord_x, coord_y)
        if res.status == 'ok':
            break
        else:
            print('go_to status:', res.status, ', sleep 10 ...')
            sleep(10)
    self.set_throttle(1)
    while self.get_distance_to(coord_x, coord_y) > 0.1:
        pass

def goto_station(self, _goto_station, throttle=1):
    print(self.name, 'goto ->', _goto_station)
    while True:
        res = self.go_to_station(_goto_station)
        if res.status == 'ok':
            break
        else:
            print('go_to_station status:', res.status, ', sleep 10 ...')
            sleep(10)
    self.set_throttle(throttle)
    while self.current_station() != _goto_station:
        pass

def cargo_unload(self):
    print('cargo_unload')
    cargo_contents = self.cargo.contents()
    for item in cargo_contents:
        print(item, cargo_contents[item])
        print(self.cargo.unload(item, cargo_contents[item]))


def battery_charging(self):
    while self.battery.percent() != 1:
        sleep(2)


def oil_charging(self):
    while self.oil_tank.percent() != 1:
        sleep(2)

#############################################


def set_home_stations(base_name):
    print(set_home_stations)
    outpost_network = get_component("outpost_network")
    #base_name = 'глубинный'

    drone_station = None
    drone_service_station = None
    for post in outpost_network.outposts():
        if post.name == base_name:
            #print(post.buildings())
            for building in post.buildings():
                if building.type_id == 'drone_station':
                    print(base_name, building.type_id, building.id)
                    drone_station = building.id
                if building.type_id == 'drone_service_station':
                    print(base_name, building.type_id, building.id)
                    drone_service_station = building.id

    return drone_station, drone_service_station



def bio_extraction(self, drone_station, drone_service_station, warehouse, biomas_points: list):

    #drone_station, drone_service_station = set_home_stations(base_name)
    #print(drone_station, drone_service_station)

    warehouse_comp = get_component(warehouse)

    goto_station(self, drone_station)
    while self.cargo.count() != 0:
        cargo_unload(self)

    goto_station(self, drone_service_station)
    battery_charging(self)

    while True:
        for point in biomas_points:
            for n in range(point[2]):
                goto(self, point[0], point[1])
                self.bio_extractor.extract()
                goto_station(self, drone_station)

                while self.cargo.count() != 0:
                    cargo_unload(self)
                    sleep(5)

                if self.battery.percent() < 0.2:
                    goto_station(self, drone_service_station)
                    battery_charging(self)

        #while warehouse_comp.total() > 100:
            #sleep(10)


def bio_extraction_smart(
    self,
    drone_station,
    drone_service,
    biom_storages,
    bio_points
):

    #_cooling_coord = []

    while True:
        print()

        goto_station(self, drone_service)
        battery_charging(self)

        goto_station(self, drone_station)
        while self.cargo.count() != 0:
            cargo_unload(self)

        # какой споры меньше всего на складе
        items_stor_count = {}
        for item_stor in biom_storages.keys():
            warehouse_id = get_component(biom_storages[item_stor])
            items_stor_count[item_stor] = warehouse_id.count(item_stor)
        print(items_stor_count)
        item_name_min_count = min(items_stor_count, key=items_stor_count.get)

        print('меньше всего:', item_name_min_count, items_stor_count[item_name_min_count])

        # если больше, то спим
        if items_stor_count[item_name_min_count] > 500:
            print('sleep ...')
            sleep(60)
            break

        # выбор поинта для сбора
        biome = get_component(drone_station).outpost.biome
        print(biome)

        #[[130, 310], 'salt_crust', 48, 'Соляная корка', 'coastal', 'uncommon']

        _good_points = []
        for point in data.scan_life_big_list:
            if point[4] == biome and point[1] == item_name_min_count:
                _good_points.append(point)
        #print('_good_points', _good_points)

        random_point  = random.choice(_good_points)
        print('random_point', random_point)
        coord = random_point[0]

        goto(self, coord[0], coord[1])
        print(self.position())

        while True:
            res = self.bio_extractor.extract()
            print(res)
            match res.status:
                case "cooling":
                    #_cooling_coord = [coord[0], coord[1]]
                    #print('_cooling_coord', _cooling_coord)
                    break
                case "busy":
                    sleep(10)
                case _:
                    break


def salt(self):
    #   дроны забирауют только у буровых или свинцового контейнера !!!!!!!!!!!!!
    water_pumps = [

        ['water_pump_1', [160, -330]],
        ['water_pump_2', [-30, -470]],
        ['water_pump_3', [10, -440]],
        ['water_pump_4', [350, 180]],
        ['water_pump_5', [640, 290]],
        ['water_pump_5', [610, -530]],

    ]

    if self.oil_tank.percent() < 0.3:
        goto_station(self, 'drone_service_1')
        oil_charging(self)

    goto_station(self, 'drone_station_lrg_1')
    cargo_unload(self)


    while True:

        if self.oil_tank.percent() < 0.3:
            goto_station(self, 'drone_service_1')
            oil_charging(self)

        water_pump_data = random.choice(water_pumps)
        water_pump_comp = get_component(water_pump_data[0])
        if water_pump_comp.output.count() > 15:
            goto(self, water_pump_data[1][0], water_pump_data[1][1])

import storages_frozen
import ioslots
import random



def random_automator():

    #список автоматоров
    print('список автоматоров')
    crop_automators = []
    for machine in get_component('outpost_home').harvesting_machines():
        if machine.type_id == 'crop_automator':
            #get_component(machine.id)
            crop_automators.append(machine.id)
    print(crop_automators)

    # выбрать автоматор
    while True:
        print('выбрать автоматор')

        automator_id = random.choice(crop_automators)
        automator_comp = get_component(automator_id)
        _count = automator_comp.output.count()

        if _count > 1000:
            print(automator_id, _count, 'качаем с него')
            return automator_id
            #break

        sleep(30)

#['crop_automator_1', 'crop_automator_2', 'crop_automator_3', 'crop_automator_4', 'crop_automator_5', 'crop_automator_6', 'crop_automator_7', 'crop_automator_8', 'crop_automator_9']


def worker(self, _craft, automator_id):

    print('WORKER start', _craft)

    #print(self.get_recipe_inputs())
    #{'forage': 100, 'cold_spores': 1, 'hot_spores': 1}
    for item, count in self.get_recipe_inputs().items():
        if item == 'forage':
            #automator_id = random_automator()
            #crop_automator_1 = get_component('crop_automator_1')

            #automator_id = 'crop_automator_1'
            print(self.input.connect(automator_id))
            ioslots.input_take(self, item, count)
            continue

        self.input.connect(storages_frozen.lifes[item])
        ioslots.input_take(self, item, count)

    while self.is_running():
        sleep(1)

    #for item in self.output.stacks():
        #ioslots.output_send(self, item.id, item.count)
    #ioslots.clear_output(self)

    print('WORKER end')


def get_creatures_in_base():

    outpost_network = get_component("outpost_network")

    creatures = []

    for b in outpost_network.home().buildings():
        if b.type_id == 'habitat':
            #print(b.id)
            habitat = get_component(b.id)
            #print(habitat.species())
            creatures.append(habitat.species())

    return creatures


def make_all_feeds(self):

    if self.input.count():
        print(self.input.flush())

    print(self.output.connect('large_warehouse_14'))

    output_stacks = self.output.stacks()
    if output_stacks:
        for item in self.output.stacks():
            ioslots.output_send(self, item.id, item.count)

    creatures_in_base = get_creatures_in_base()
    warehouse = get_component('large_warehouse_14')
    automator_id = random_automator()

    while True:

        automator_comp = get_component(automator_id)
        if automator_comp.output.count() < 500:
            automator_id = random_automator()

        print('===================')

        warehouse_stacks = ioslots.stacks_to_dict(warehouse.stacks())

        check_feeds_count = []


        for creature in creatures_in_base:
            #print(creature)

            _feed = f'feed_{creature}'
            #print(_feed)

            _feed_warehouse_count = warehouse_stacks.get(_feed, 0)
            if _feed_warehouse_count < 100:
                check_feeds_count.append([_feed, _feed_warehouse_count])


        #print(check_feeds_count)
        if check_feeds_count:
            min_feed = min(check_feeds_count, key=lambda x: x[1])
            _craft = f'craft_{min_feed[0]}'
            print(_craft)
            print(self.set_recipe(_craft))
            worker(self, _craft, automator_id)

        sleep(1)


def main_comms(self):

    self.clear_recipe()

    if self.input.count():
        print(self.input.flush())

    print(self.output.connect('large_warehouse_14'))

    output_stacks = self.output.stacks()
    if output_stacks:
        for item in self.output.stacks():
            ioslots.output_send(self, item.id, item.count)

    #creatures_in_base = get_creatures_in_base()
    #warehouse = get_component('large_warehouse_14')
    #automator_id = random_automator()



    comms = get_component("comms")

    automator_id = random_automator()

    while True:

        received = comms.wait(f"jobs.feed")
        if received.status != "ok":
            print(received)
            print('COMM ERR', received.message)
            break
        job = received.packet
        print()
        print('----------------')
        print("COMM in Received", job.id, job.value)


        habitat = get_component(job.value)
        _feed = habitat.required_feed()
        _craft = f'craft_{_feed}'

        print(self.set_recipe(_craft))

        automator_comp = get_component(automator_id)
        if automator_comp.output.count() < 500:
            automator_id = random_automator()


        worker(self, _craft, automator_id)

        print(self.output.connect(job.value))
        ioslots.output_send(self, _feed, self.output.count())

        self.clear_recipe()

        sleep(1)

'''

Пыльные и грозовые бури

1) пылевых бурь - между 5 биомами
Пыльная буря оставляет 14–28 единиц сырого урана на 48 часов по мировым часам.

Подходящая грозовая буря оставляет 2–4 единицы грозового стекла на 96 часов по мировым часам.

import time

power = get_component("power_control")

device_check = ['temp_heater', 'oxygen_generator', 'pressure_generator']

#{'temp_heater': {1: 7, 2: 2},
#'oxygen_generator': {1: 7, 2: 2},
#'pressure_generator': {1: 6, 2: 4}}


def collector(data: dict):
    for grid in power.grids():
        #grid.outpost_ids[0]
        #print(grid.anchor_id, grid.generated, grid.consumed, grid.net)

        outpost = grid.outpost_ids[0]
        outpost = get_component(outpost).name()
        data[outpost] = {}

        for member in grid.members:
            if member.type_id in device_check:
                _device_tier = get_component(member.id).tier()

                #print(member.outpost_id, member.type_id, member.id, _device_tier)

                if member.type_id in data[outpost]:
                    _count = data[outpost][member.type_id].get(_device_tier)
                    if _count:
                        data[outpost][member.type_id][_device_tier] = _count + 1
                    else:
                        data[outpost][member.type_id][_device_tier] = 1
                else:
                    data[outpost][member.type_id] = {_device_tier: 1}

                #value = data.setdefault(member.type_id, {})
                #if data.get(member.type_id)


        #oxygen_generator: {1: 6, 2:2, 3:0}



def show(data):
    #data = {'outpost_1': {'temp_heater': {1: 2}, 'oxygen_generator': {1: 2}, 'pressure_generator': {1: 3}}, 'outpost_2': {'temp_heater': {1: 1}, 'oxygen_generator': {2: 2, 1: 2}, 'pressure_generator': {2: 2}}, 'outpost_3': {'temp_heater': {1: 3, 2: 2}, 'oxygen_generator': {1: 1}, 'pressure_generator': {1: 2, 2: 2}}, 'outpost_home': {'temp_heater': {1: 1}, 'oxygen_generator': {1: 2}, 'pressure_generator': {1: 1}}}

    groups = [('oxygen_generator', 'oxygen'),
              ('temp_heater', 'heater'),
              ('pressure_generator', 'pressure')]

    # home первым, остальные по порядку
    names = ['Nocturna Base'] + [n for n in data if n != 'Nocturna Base']

    tiers = sorted({t for o in data.values() for k, _ in groups for t in o.get(k, {})})

    cw = 4
    gw = len(tiers) * cw + len(tiers) - 1
    nw = max(len(n) for n in [*names, 'итого'])
    line = '-' * (nw + 3 + len(groups) * gw + (len(groups) - 1) * 3)

    def row(name, cells):
        body = ' | '.join(' '.join(f'{c:>{cw}}' for c in g) for g in cells)
        return f'{name:<{nw}} | {body}'

    print(f'{"":<{nw}} | ' + ' | '.join(f'{title:^{gw}}' for _, title in groups))
    print(row('', [[f'T{t}' for t in tiers]] * len(groups)))
    print(line)

    print(names)

    for name in names:
        o = data[name]
        print(row(name, [[o.get(k, {}).get(t, '') for t in tiers] for k, _ in groups]))

    print(line)
    print(row('итого', [[sum(o.get(k, {}).get(t, 0) for o in data.values()) or '' for t in tiers]
                        for k, _ in groups]))

import drones



def update_notebook(notebook_name, data):
    notebook = get_component("notebook")

    # открыть словарь и добавить новый, оператор |
    notebook_data = notebook.get(notebook_name) | data
    print(notebook.set(notebook_name, notebook_data))


def poi_scan():

    def nearest_point(not_scaned_points):

        _distance_to_point = {}

        for point in not_scaned_points:
            _distance = self.get_distance_to(point.x, point.y)
            _distance_to_point[_distance] = point

        next_nearest_point = _distance_to_point[sorted(_distance_to_point.keys())[0]]
        print(next_nearest_point)
        return next_nearest_point

    # <PointOfInterest: x=-220, y=-390, scanned=True, kind='inert'>
    #<PointOfInterest: x=-450, y=-310, scanned=False, kind='unknown'>
    #<PointOfInterest: x=130, y=660, scanned=True, kind='biomass'>

    _done_list = []
    _black_list = []
    _life_list = []

    while True:

        print()
        print(1)

        _work_points = []

        scan = get_component("nocturna").points_of_interest()
        for point in scan:
            if (point.kind in ['biomass', 'unknown']) and ([point.x, point.y] not in _done_list):
                _work_points.append(point)

        print(2)

        if not _work_points:
            print('not not_scaned_points')
            break

        next_nearest_point = nearest_point(_work_points)
        #print(next_nearest_point)

        drones.goto(self, next_nearest_point.x, next_nearest_point.y)

        print(4)

        scan_res = self.bio_scanner.scan().scan
        print(scan_res)
        if scan_res.is_empty:
            _black_list.append([next_nearest_point.x, next_nearest_point.y])

        else:
            for life in scan_res.life_forms:
                print(life)
                _life_list.append([[next_nearest_point.x, next_nearest_point.y], life.type, life.tons])


        print(5)

        _done_list.append([next_nearest_point.x, next_nearest_point.y])
        print('_done_list =', _done_list)
        print('_black_list =', _black_list)
        print('_life_list =', _life_list)

        if self.battery.percent() < 0.3:
            drones.goto_station(self, 'drone_service_1')
            drones.battery_charging(self)

panels.py×
comms = get_component("comms")


def fabricator_recipes(panel, fabricator_id):

    component = get_component(fabricator_id)
    recipes = component.list_recipes()
    recipes_list = {}

    def comms_send(channel, msg):
        sent = comms.send(channel, msg)
        print(sent)
        if sent.status != "ok":
            print(sent.message)


    def get_item_name_from_recipe(recp):
        item = component.find_recipe(recp)
        return item.output_item

    def make_oneline_recipes():
        for tier_n in range(0, 6):
            #print()
            #print('Tier', tier_n)
            for recipe in recipes:
                #print(recipe)
                if recipe.tier == tier_n:
                    #print(recipe)
                    rus_name = recipe.name[recipe.name.find('→')+2:].capitalize()
                    #print(tier_n, rus_name, recipe.id)
                    recipes_list[recipe.id] = {
                        'item_name': get_item_name_from_recipe(recipe.id),
                        'rus_name': rus_name,
                        'tier_n': tier_n
                    }
                    #print(f'{_name:<30}  {recipe.id:<30} {recipe.inputs:<30} {recipe.fluid_inputs}')

    make_oneline_recipes()

    #print(recipes_list)

    tiers_n = {}

    for t in range(1, 5):
        #t += 1
        #print(t)
        tiers_n[t] = [key for key, value in recipes_list.items()if value['tier_n'] == t]

    #print(tiers_n)

    icon_size = 11

    active_recipe = None

    while True:
        panel.clear()

        for tier_n in tiers_n:
            # 1 2 3 4

            if tiers_n[tier_n]:
                if tier_n == 1:
                    x = 40
                else:
                    x = 40 + 200 * (tier_n - 1)

                for n, craft_item in enumerate(tiers_n[tier_n]):
                    if n == 0:
                        y = 10
                    else:
                        y = 10 + n * (icon_size + 2)   # тут менять

                    #panel.draw_text(x=x, y=y, text=recipes_list[craft_item]['rus_name'], size=icon_size-2, color="text-muted")
                    panel.draw_text(x=x, y=y, text=recipes_list[craft_item]['item_name'], size=icon_size-2, color="text-muted")

                    if panel.icon_button(
                        craft_item, x=x-25, y=y-7,
                        size=icon_size+6, item_id=recipes_list[craft_item]['item_name']
                    ):
                        print(craft_item)
                        active_recipe = craft_item



        # блок активации

        card_x = 820
        card_y = 20
        #panel.card(card_x, card_y, 300, 100, title=fabricator_id)

        #panel.draw_text(x=card_x+10, y=card_y, text=fabricator_id, size=10, color="text-muted")
        panel.label(x=card_x, y=card_y, text=fabricator_id, style='title')

        panel.draw_text(x=card_x, y=card_y+30, text=f'recipe: {active_recipe}', size=8, color="text-muted")

        text_field_n = panel.text_field(
            key='text_field',
            x=card_x, y=card_y + 50, w=50,
            default='1',
            placeholder='* n',
            #label='count',
            size=24
        )

        if panel.button(
            key='button1',
            x=card_x, y= card_y + 90, w=50, h=30,
            label='Run'
        ):
            #pass
            comms_send(f'jobs.{component.name}', {'recipe': active_recipe, 'count': int(text_field_n)})


        #прогресс полукругом
        _progress = component.get_progress()
        panel.gauge(
            card_x + 20, card_y + 200, radius=20,
            fraction=_progress,
            #label=str(_progress * 100)
        )
        #значек активного рецепта
        #print(component.get_recipe()[6:])
        panel.draw_icon(card_x + 6, card_y + 184, item_id=component.get_recipe()[6:], size=30)

        #статус
        status = component.get_status_report()
        if status:
            status_msg = f'status: {status.message}'
        else:
            status_msg = f'status: None'

        panel.draw_text(x=card_x, y=card_y+160, text=status_msg, size=8, color="text-muted")
rover_1.py×
def check_pos(coord_x, coord_y):

    dopusk = 0.1

    get_position = self.nav.get_position()

    #print(type(get_position))
    #print(get_position.x, get_position.x)

    if (- dopusk < coord_x - get_position.x < dopusk) and (- dopusk < coord_y - get_position.y < dopusk):
        return True
    else:
        return False


def move(coord_x, coord_y):

    #print(self.nav.get_distance_to(30, 30))

#    print(self.nav.set_target(30, 30))
    #print(self.nav.set_throttle(1))

    while not check_pos(coord_x, coord_y):
        #print(1)
        self.nav.set_target(coord_x, coord_y)
        self.nav.set_throttle(1)

    self.nav.brake()


def scan_survey():
    scan = self.sonar.scan()
    print(scan)

    survey = self.sonar.survey(scan.sites[0])
    print(survey)

#scan_survey()

def drill():
    for i in range(10):
        self.drill.mine()

def unload():
    #print(self.output.connect('storage_bin_1'))

    #print(self.cargo.stacks())
    for stack in self.cargo.stacks():

        self.output.send(stack.id, stack.count)

def battery():
    while self.battery.level() != 1:
        sleep(2)


scan = get_component("nocturna").points_of_interest()

def road_scan():
    distance_from = 200

    for point in scan:

        black_list = [
            [-120, 70],
            [60, 110]
        ]

        if [point.x, point.y] in black_list:
            print('black list skip', [point.x, point.y])
            continue

        #print(point)
        if not point.scanned and self.nav.get_distance_to(point.x, point.y) < distance_from:

            print('goto scan: ', point)

            move(point.x, point.y)
            scan_survey()

            #if self.battery.level() < 0.3:
            move(0, 0)
            battery()



def main():
    #coord_x, coord_y = 0, 0
    #coord_x, coord_y = 30, 30

    #move(coord_x, coord_y)
    #scan_survey()
    #drill()

    #unload()
    move(0, 0)
    battery()

    #road_scan()

main()

import time

power = get_component("power_control")
outpost_network = get_component("outpost_network")

device_check = ['temp_heater', 'oxygen_generator', 'pressure_generator']

#{'temp_heater': {1: 7, 2: 2},
#'oxygen_generator': {1: 7, 2: 2},

data = {}

def collector(data: dict):
    for outpost in outpost_network.outposts():

        #outpost = grid.outpost_ids[0]
        #outpost = get_component(outpost).name()
        data[outpost.name] = {}

        for member in outpost.buildings():
            if member.type_id in device_check:
                _device_tier = get_component(member.id).tier()
                #print(member.outpost_id, member.type_id, member.id, _device_tier)

                if member.type_id in data[outpost.name]:
                    _count = data[outpost.name][member.type_id].get(_device_tier)
                    if _count:
                        data[outpost.name][member.type_id][_device_tier] = _count + 1
                    else:
                        data[outpost.name][member.type_id][_device_tier] = 1
                else:
                    data[outpost.name][member.type_id] = {_device_tier: 1}

panel_8.py×
import time

power = get_component("power_control")

device_check = ['temp_heater', 'oxygen_generator', 'pressure_generator']

#{'temp_heater': {1: 7, 2: 2},
#'oxygen_generator': {1: 7, 2: 2},
#'pressure_generator': {1: 6, 2: 4}}


def collector(data: dict):
    for grid in power.grids():
        #grid.outpost_ids[0]
        #print(grid.anchor_id, grid.generated, grid.consumed, grid.net)

        outpost = grid.outpost_ids[0]
        data[outpost] = {}

        for member in grid.members:
            if member.type_id in device_check:
                _device_tier = get_component(member.id).tier()

                #print(member.outpost_id, member.type_id, member.id, _device_tier)

                if member.type_id in data[outpost]:
                    _count = data[outpost][member.type_id].get(_device_tier)
                    if _count:
                        data[outpost][member.type_id][_device_tier] = _count + 1
                    else:
                        data[outpost][member.type_id][_device_tier] = 1
                else:
                    data[outpost][member.type_id] = {_device_tier: 1}

                #value = data.setdefault(member.type_id, {})
                #if data.get(member.type_id)


        #oxygen_generator: {1: 6, 2:2, 3:0}


def show(data):
    data = {'outpost_1': {'temp_heater': {1: 2}, 'oxygen_generator': {1: 2}, 'pressure_generator': {1: 3}}, 'outpost_2': {'temp_heater': {1: 1}, 'oxygen_generator': {2: 2, 1: 2}, 'pressure_generator': {2: 2}}, 'outpost_3': {'temp_heater': {1: 3, 2: 2}, 'oxygen_generator': {1: 1}, 'pressure_generator': {1: 2, 2: 2}}, 'outpost_home': {'temp_heater': {1: 1}, 'oxygen_generator': {1: 2}, 'pressure_generator': {1: 1}}}

    groups = [('oxygen_generator', 'oxygen'),
              ('temp_heater', 'heater'),
              ('pressure_generator', 'pressure')]

    # home первым, остальные по порядку
    names = ['outpost_home'] + [n for n in data if n != 'outpost_home']

    tiers = sorted({t for o in data.values() for k, _ in groups for t in o.get(k, {})})

    cw = 4
    gw = len(tiers) * cw + len(tiers) - 1
    nw = max(len(n) for n in [*names, 'итого'])
    line = '-' * (nw + 3 + len(groups) * gw + (len(groups) - 1) * 3)

    def row(name, cells):
        body = ' | '.join(' '.join(f'{c:>{cw}}' for c in g) for g in cells)
        return f'{name:<{nw}} | {body}'

    print(f'{"":<{nw}} | ' + ' | '.join(f'{title:^{gw}}' for _, title in groups))
    print(row('', [[f'T{t}' for t in tiers]] * len(groups)))
    print(line)

    for name in names:
        o = data[name]
        print(row(name, [[o.get(k, {}).get(t, '') for t in tiers] for k, _ in groups]))

    print(line)
    print(row('итого', [[sum(o.get(k, {}).get(t, 0) for o in data.values()) or '' for t in tiers]
                        for k, _ in groups]))



def main():

    data = {}
    collector(data)

    #print(data)

    show(data)
    #print(data)



main()

import storages
import storages_exotic
import ioslots

#print(self.list_recipes())

#for i in self.list_recipes():
    #print(i)

#self.set_recipe(recipe_id)



exotics = ['refine_sulfur_gas', 'refine_cryofluid']


def main():

    # смола
    print(self.input.connect('large_warehouse_2'))

    for exotic in exotics:
        print(exotic)
        self.set_recipe(exotic)
        active_recipe = self.find_recipe(exotic)

        if 'gas_in' in active_recipe.fluid_inputs:
            # газ
            print(self.gas_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[active_recipe.input_fluid]))
            print(self.gas_out.connect(storages_exotic.container[active_recipe.output_fluid]))

        else:
            # жидкость
            print(self.liquid_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[active_recipe.input_fluid]))
            print(self.liquid_out.connect(storages_exotic.container[active_recipe.output_fluid]))

        output_container_id = storages_exotic.container[active_recipe.output_fluid]
        output_container_comp = get_component(output_container_id)

        #output_fill_pct = output_container_comp.fill_pct()
        output_level = output_container_comp.level()
        while output_level < 800:

            self.set_status(f'{output_container_id}: {output_level}/{output_container_comp.capacity()}')
            ioslots.input_take(self, 'tar', active_recipe.inputs['tar'] * 3)

            while self.is_running():
                sleep(1)

            output_level = output_container_comp.level()


        self.clear_recipe()
        print('done')
        sleep(5)

#main()


print(self.input.flush())

def auto_main():
    while True:
        main()
        sleep(10)

atmo = get_component("atmosphere")
clock = get_component("clock")
notebook = get_component("notebook")

#cols = list(zip(*atmo.values()))
#s1, s2, s3, s4, s5, s6 = cols


atmo = notebook.get("atmo")
#print(type(atmo))
#print(atmo.keys())




names = ['co2', 'o2', 'n2', 'pressure', 'temperature', 'heat']

def make_data():

    data = {
        'co2': [],
        'o2': [],
        'n2': [],
        'pressure': [],
        'temperature': [],
        'heat': []
    }

    for n , key in enumerate(atmo):
        if n == 0:
            prev_day = atmo[key]
            continue

        for n_name, name in enumerate(names):
            data[name].append(atmo[key][n_name] - prev_day[n_name])
        prev_day = atmo[key]

    return data

def main():

    while True:

        data = make_data()

        panel.clear()
        #co2, o2, n2, pressure, temperature, heat = make_data()
        for n, key in enumerate(data):
            if n == 0:
                y = 10
            else:
                y = n * 80 + 10

            panel.draw_text(10, y+5, names[n], 10)
            panel.spark_line(x=90, y=y, w=270, h=60, values=data[key], axis=True)
        sleep(350)

main()

ioslots.py×
import storages
#ioslots

def stacks_to_dict(stacks):
    _data = {}
    for item in stacks:
        #print(item)
        _data[item.id] = _data.get(item.id, 0) + item.count
    return _data

def have_inside(stacks, need_item, need_count=1):
    for stack in stacks:
        if stack.id == need_item and stack.count >= need_count:
            return True
    return False


#########################################################


def _transfer(self, action, op, src, dst, item, count):

    #res = op(dst, item, count)

    if action in ['input_take', 'output_send', 'byproduct_send']:
        res = op(item, count)
    elif action in ['input_eject']:
        res = op(dst, item, count)
    elif action in ['storage_transfer_to']:
        res = op(src, dst, item, count)
    else:
        warn('wrong action', action)

    if res.status == 'ok':
        print(f'{action}: {src} -> {dst}, {item} [{res.moved}/{res.requested}]')
    elif res.status == 'busy':
        print(f'busy {action}: {src} -> {dst}, {item} {count} ... sleep')
        sleep(3)
        _transfer(self, action, op, src, dst, item, count)
    else:
        warn(res)


def input_take(self, item, count):
    _transfer(self, 'input_take', self.input.take, self.input.connected_to(), self.name, item, count)

def output_send(self, item, count):
    _transfer(self, 'output_send', self.output.send, self.name, self.output.connected_to(), item, count)

def byproduct_send(self, item, count):
    _transfer(self, 'byproduct_send', self.byproduct.send, self.name, self.byproduct.connected_to(), item, count)

# в цель без смены коннекта
#.eject(destination: str, item_id: str, count:
def input_eject(self, target, item, count):
#    .eject(destination: str, item_id: str, count: int,
    _transfer(self, 'input_eject', self.input.eject, self.name, target, item, count)

def storage_transfer_to(src: str, target:str, item:str, count:int):
    #st = get_component(src)
    #_transfer(self, 'storage_transfer_to', st.transfer_to, src, target, item, count)
    _transfer(self, 'storage_transfer_to', self.transfer_to, self.name, target, item, count)

'''
def print_transfer_result(self, action, item, res, target=None):
    if res.status == 'ok':

        if action == 'input_take':
            _from = self.input.connected_to()
            _to = self.name
        if action == 'output_send':
            _from = self.name
            _to = self.input.connected_to()
        print(f'{action}: {_from} -> {_to}, {item} [{res.moved}/{res.requested}]')
    else:
        warn(res)
def input_take(self, item, count):
    res = self.input.take(item, count)
    print_transfer_result(self, 'input_take', item, res)

def output_send(self, item, count):
    res = self.output.send(item, count)
    print_transfer_result(self, 'output_send', item, res)


def storage_transfer_to(self, target, item, count):
    res = self.output.send(item, count)
    print_transfer_result('storage_transfer_to', item, res, target=target)
'''
#########################################################


def unload_all(self):
    print('unload_all')
    for item in self.output.stacks():
        output_send(self, item.id, item.count)

def clear_input(self):
    print('clear_input')
    for item in self.input.stacks():
        input_eject(self, storages.get_storages(item.id), item.id, item.count)

def clear_output(self):
    print('clear_output')
    for item in self.output.stacks():
        output_send(self, item.id, item.count)

def clear_byproduct(self):
    print('clear_byproduct')
    for item in self.byproduct.stacks():
        byproduct_send(self, item.id, item.count)

def storage_all_transfer_to(src, dst):

    if isinstance(src, str):
        src = get_component(src)

    print('storage_all_transfer_to')
    for item in self.stacks():
        storage_transfer_to(dst, item.id, item.count)

    need_list = []

        if life in storages.frozen:
            continue

        #print(life, home_warehouse_count)

def craft_seed(seed, count):
    print('==========================')

    if self.input.stacks():
        for item in self.input.stacks():
            ioslots.input_eject(self, storages_frozen.lifes[item.id], item.id, item.count)
    if self.output.stacks():
        for item in self.output.stacks():
            print(self.output.send(item.id, item.count))

    print()

    for recipe in self.recipes():
        if recipe.seed_id == seed:
            req_blend = recipe.blend
            break

    for n in range(count):

        print(n + 1, seed, req_blend)

        for item in req_blend:
            self.input.connect(storages_frozen.lifes[item])
            ioslots.input_take(self, item, 1)
            #print(self.input.take(item, 1))

        res = self.combine(req_blend)
        if res.status == "seed_found":

            #print('clear_output')
            for item in self.output.stacks():
                ioslots.output_send(self, item.id, item.count)
                #self.output.send(item.id, item.count)
        else:
            print(n + 1, seed, req_blend, res.status)
        print()

import storages
import ioslots

seed_warehouse = get_component('large_warehouse_5')


greed_n = [str(n) for n in range(1, 25)]
greed_l = ['A', 'B','C','D','E','F','G','H']

#greed_n = [str(n) for n in range(1, 25)]

greed_l_dict = {'A':1, 'B':2,'C':3,'D':4,'E':5,'F':6,'G':7,'H':8}


def chek_heat(self):
    while self.get_heat() > 90:
        self.set_status(f'cooling down ...')
        sleep(5)

def make_route(self, target_coord):
    # строит дорогу до координат,
    # сначало вырванивает горизонт, затем вертикаль

    #letter
    #number

    self_coord = self.get_position()
    self_coord_ltr = self_coord[:1]
    self_coord_nbr = int(self_coord[1:])

    #print(self_coord, self_coord_ltr, greed_l_dict[self_coord_ltr], self_coord_nbr)

    target_coord_ltr = target_coord[:1]
    target_coord_nbr = int(target_coord[1:])

    road = []

    if target_coord == self_coord:
        return []

    def go_goris():
        # выравнивание горизонта

        if self_coord_nbr == target_coord_nbr:
            return

        if self_coord_nbr > target_coord_nbr:
            _n = self_coord_nbr - target_coord_nbr
            for n in range(_n):
                road.append(self_coord_ltr + str(self_coord_nbr - n - 1))

        if self_coord_nbr < target_coord_nbr:
            _n = target_coord_nbr - self_coord_nbr
            for n in range(_n):
                road.append(self_coord_ltr + str(self_coord_nbr + n + 1))

    def go_vert():
        # выравнивание вертикали

        #print(self_coord_ltr, target_coord_ltr)

        if self_coord_ltr == target_coord_ltr:
            return

        if greed_l_dict[self_coord_ltr] > greed_l_dict[target_coord_ltr]:
            #print(1)
            _n = greed_l_dict[self_coord_ltr] - greed_l_dict[target_coord_ltr]
            #print(_n)
            for n in range(_n):
                _ltr = greed_l[greed_l_dict[self_coord_ltr] - n - 2]
                road.append(_ltr + str(target_coord_nbr))

        if greed_l_dict[self_coord_ltr] < greed_l_dict[target_coord_ltr]:
            _n = greed_l_dict[target_coord_ltr] - greed_l_dict[self_coord_ltr]
            for n in range(_n):
                _ltr = greed_l[greed_l_dict[self_coord_ltr] + n]
                road.append(_ltr + str(target_coord_nbr))

    go_goris()
    go_vert()

    return road


def goto(self, coord):
    road = make_route(self, coord)
    for move in road:
        chek_heat(self)
        self.set_status(f'move to: {coord}')
        self.move(move)
    self.clear_status()


def water(self):
    if self.water_level() == 0:
        print(self.refill_water())
    print(self.water())


def plant_seed(self, seed):
    print(seed_warehouse.transfer_to('inventory', seed, 1))
    print(self.load_seed(seed))
    print(self.plant(seed))
    match seed:
        case 'seed_sunpetal':
            print(self.light())
        case 'seed_dewmoss':
            water(self)


def harv(self):
    while True:
        cell_info = self.cell(self.get_position())
        res = self.harvest()
        print('harvest:', cell_info.plant, ', forage:', cell_info.forage, ', status:', res.status)
        cell_info = self.cell(self.get_position())
        if not cell_info.plant:
            break
    #inv = get_component('inventory')
    #print(inv.transfer_to('large_warehouse_2', 'forage', inv.count('forage')))
    #ioslots.storage_transfer_to('inventory', 'large_warehouse_2', 'forage', inv.count('forage'))
    #st = get_component('inventory')
    #print(st.transfer_to('large_warehouse_2', 'forage', cell_info.forage))

            self.set_status(f'{output_container_id}: {output_level}/{output_container_comp.capacity()}')

            while self.is_running():
                sleep(1)

            output_level = output_container_comp.level()


        self.clear_recipe()
        print('done')
        sleep(5)

def set_earth_order():

    if ORDERS == 'main':
        orders = get_component("orders").list_orders()

    if ORDERS == 'weekly':
        orders = get_component("orders").list_weekly_orders()
        #orders.reverse()

    for o in orders:
        if o.status != 'completed':

            print(o.id, o.name, o.requires, o.status, sep=', ')

            req_ready = []

            for req in o.requires:
                storage = get_component(storages.get_storages(req))
                if storage.count(req) >= o.requires[req]:
                    req_ready.append(True)
                else:
                    req_ready.append(False)

            if all(req_ready):
                set_o = self.set_order(o.id)
                print(set_o)
                return True

    return False

def worker(need_count):

    #inputs={'raw_uranium': 4, 'lead_plate': 2}

    for item, count in self.get_recipe_inputs().items():
        print(item, count)

        if item == 'raw_uranium':
            self.input.connect(storages.storages['raw_uranium'])
            ioslots.input_take(self, item, count * need_count)

        if item == 'lead_plate':
            self.input.connect(storages.storages['lead_plate'])
            ioslots.input_take(self, item, count * need_count)

    while self.is_running():
        sleep(1)


    for item in self.output.stacks():
        self.output.connect(storages.storages[item.id])
        ioslots.output_send(self, item.id, item.count)

def extract_data(data):
    #storm_1269|3|4|300|300
    data_list = data.split('|')





def get_lightning_signals():
    print('get_lightning_signals')

    signals_data = []

    w_station = get_component('weather_station_7')

    if not w_station.signal_receiver.transmissions():
        print('no signals')
        return None

    for n, signal in enumerate(w_station.signal_receiver.transmissions()):
        #print(signal)

        if 'storm_dust_' not in signal.event_id:

            _checksum_ord = sum(ord(char) for char in signal.data)
            _checksum_check = True if signal.checksum == _checksum_ord else False

            if _checksum_check:
                if signal.data.split('|') not in signals_data:
                    signals_data.append(signal.data.split('|'))
                #signals_data.append([signal.data, signal.channel, signal.source_station_id])

    #print(signals_data)
    signals_data.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
    print('signals_data', signals_data)
    print()

    x = 0
    y = 0

    for item in signals_data:
        print(item)
        x += int(item[3])
        y += int(item[4])

    print(x, y)
    return x, y

def get_dust_signals():
    print('get_dust_signals')
    weather_stations = [
        'weather_station_7',
        'weather_station_3',
        'weather_station_4',
        'weather_station_6',
        'weather_station_5',
    ]

    signals_data = []

    for station in weather_stations:
        w_station = get_component(station)
        for n, signal in enumerate(w_station.signal_receiver.transmissions()):
            #print(signal)

            if 'storm_dust_' in signal.event_id:



                _checksum_ord = sum(ord(char) for char in signal.data)
                _checksum_check = True if signal.checksum == _checksum_ord else False

                if _checksum_check:
                    if signal.data.split('|') not in signals_data:
                        signals_data.append(signal.data.split('|'))

                #if signal.channel != 'broadcast':
                #if signal.data not in signals_data:
                    #signals_data.append((signal.data, signal.source_station_id))
                    #signals_data.append([signal.data, signal.channel, signal.source_station_id])

    print('signals_data', signals_data)

    if not signals_data:
        print('no signals')
        return None

    signals_data.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
    print('signals_data', signals_data)
    print()

    x = 0
    y = 0

    for item in signals_data:
        print(item)
        x += int(item[3])

lattice.py×
from collections import deque

def solve_lattice(grid):
    W, H = grid.width(), grid.height()
    start = grid.start()
    opened = {}          # (x, y) -> reading
    mines = set()
    queue = deque()      # доказанно безопасные, ещё не открытые
    queued = set()

    def neighbors(x, y):
        for dx in (-1, 0, 1):
            for dy in (-1, 0, 1):
                if dx or dy:
                    nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy
                    if 0 <= nx < W and 0 <= ny < H:
                        yield (nx, ny)

    def push_safe(c):
        if c not in opened and c not in queued and c not in mines:
            queued.add(c)
            queue.append(c)

    def open_cell(c):
        res = grid.probe(c[0], c[1])
        if res.reading is None:
            # Не должно случиться: проверяем только доказанно безопасные.
            grid.reset()
            raise RuntimeError(f"Сбой на {c}: {res.status} {res.message}")
        opened[c] = res.reading

    def constraints():
        cons = []
        for c, r in opened.items():
            unk = [n for n in neighbors(*c) if n not in opened and n not in mines]
            need = r - sum(1 for n in neighbors(*c) if n in mines)
            if unk:
                cons.append((tuple(unk), need))
        return cons

    def simple_rules():
        progress = False
        for unk, need in constraints():
            if need == 0:
                for n in unk:
                    if n not in queued:
                        push_safe(n); progress = True
            elif need == len(unk):
                for n in unk:
                    if n not in mines:
                        mines.add(n); progress = True
        return progress

    def enumerate_rules():
        """Полный перебор по связным компонентам границы."""
        cons = constraints()
        var_cons = {}
        for i, (unk, need) in enumerate(cons):
            for v in unk:
                var_cons.setdefault(v, []).append(i)
        seen, progress = set(), False
        for v0 in list(var_cons):
            if v0 in seen:
                continue
            # компонента связности (BFS), порядок обхода = порядок перебора
            order, dq = [], deque([v0]); seen.add(v0)
            while dq:
                v = dq.popleft(); order.append(v)
                for ci in var_cons[v]:
                    for w in cons[ci][0]:
                        if w not in seen:
                            seen.add(w); dq.append(w)
            comp_cons = sorted({ci for v in order for ci in var_cons[v]})
            assign = {}
            can0, can1 = set(), set()

            def ok(v):
                for ci in var_cons[v]:
                    unk, need = cons[ci]
                    m = sum(1 for u in unk if assign.get(u) == 1)
                    free = sum(1 for u in unk if u not in assign)
                    if m > need or m + free < need:
                        return False
                return True

            def rec(i):
                if i == len(order):
                    for u, val in assign.items():
                        (can1 if val else can0).add(u)
                    return
                v = order[i]
                for val in (0, 1):
                    assign[v] = val
                    if ok(v):
                        rec(i + 1)
                    del assign[v]

            rec(0)
            for v in order:
                if v in can0 and v not in can1:
                    push_safe(v); progress = True
                elif v in can1 and v not in can0:
                    if v not in mines:
                        mines.add(v); progress = True
        return progress

    push_safe(tuple(start))
    while True:
        while queue:
            open_cell(queue.popleft())
        if simple_rules() or queue:
            continue
        if enumerate_rules() or queue:
            continue
        break

    unknown = [(x, y) for y in range(H) for x in range(W)
               if (x, y) not in opened and (x, y) not in mines]
    if unknown:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Не определено ячеек: {len(unknown)}, например {unknown[:5]}")

    return [1 if (x, y) in mines else 0 for y in range(H) for x in range(W)]


node_map = solve_lattice(self.contract.grid)
assert len(node_map) == 1024
transmitter = get_component("transmitter")
transmitter.connect("earth")
transmitter.transmit(self.contract.id, node_map)

    _good_line = ''
    for n, bit in enumerate(x):
        #print(n, bit)
        if bit in ['0', '1']:

                _good_line += bit
            else:
                #print(x[n-2:n+3], False)
                continue
        #else:
            #_good_line += bit
        #print(_good_line)

    return _good_line

'''

(x AND NOT y) OR (NOT x AND y)


Сопоставь биты потоков: x[0] с y[0], x[1] с y[1] и так далее. Для каждой пары вычисли:
x y' + x' y
То же выражение: (x AND NOT y) OR (NOT x AND y)
' означает NOT, два символа рядом означают AND, а + означает OR. Для каждой пары получится один бит.

import storages
import ioslots


def worker():

    print('worker')

    #inputs={'raw_uranium': 4, 'lead_plate': 2}

    input_stacks = ioslots.stacks_to_dict(self.input.stacks())

    for item, count in self.get_recipe_inputs().items():
        #print(item, count)

        if item == 'raw_uranium' and input_stacks.get(item, 0) < count:
            self.input.connect('lead_cask_4')
            ioslots.input_take(self, item, count)

        if item == 'lead_plate' and input_stacks.get(item, 0) < count:
            self.input.connect('large_warehouse_12')
            ioslots.input_take(self, item, count)

    while self.is_running():
        sleep(1)

    for item in self.output.stacks():
        self.output.connect('lead_cask_5')
        ioslots.output_send(self, item.id, item.count)


def auto_main():
    self.set_recipe('craft_fuel_rod')
    lead_cask_2 = get_component('lead_cask_5')
    while True:
        if lead_cask_2.count('fuel_rod') < 20:
            worker()
        else:
            sleep(10)

auto_main()

import storages
import ioslots


def worker():

    print('worker')

    #inputs={'raw_uranium': 4, 'lead_plate': 2}

    input_stacks = ioslots.stacks_to_dict(self.input.stacks())

    for item, count in self.get_recipe_inputs().items():
        #print(item, count)

        if item == 'raw_uranium' and input_stacks.get(item, 0) < count:
            self.input.connect('lead_cask_4')
            ioslots.input_take(self, item, count)

        if item == 'lead_plate' and input_stacks.get(item, 0) < count:
            self.input.connect('large_warehouse_12')
            ioslots.input_take(self, item, count)

    while self.is_running():
        sleep(1)

    for item in self.output.stacks():
        self.output.connect('lead_cask_5')
        ioslots.output_send(self, item.id, item.count)


def auto_main():
    self.set_recipe('craft_fuel_rod')
    lead_cask_2 = get_component('lead_cask_5')
    while True:
        if lead_cask_2.count('fuel_rod') < 20:
            worker()
        else:
            sleep(10)

• Выбирает продукт. self.list_recipes() → self.set_recipe(recipe_id); self.get_recipe_inputs() показывает необходимые ингредиенты.
• Пополняет запас. self.input.take(item_id, n) забирает предметы из твоего хранилища; self.get_stockpile() показывает, что уже загружено.
• Забирает готовый корм. self.output.send(feed_id, n) направляет его во входной порт Вольера.
• Следит за работой. self.is_running() / self.get_progress().





def smart_comms():

    # feed_maker_1

    comms = get_component("comms")

    self.clear_status()
    self.clear_recipe()

    while True:

        self.set_status('ready')

        received = comms.wait(f"jobs.{self.name}")
        if received.status != "ok":
            print(received)
            print('COMM ERR', received.message)
            break
        job = received.packet
        #print(job)
        print()
        print('----------------')
        print("COMM in Received", job.id, job.value)

        #{'recipe': radio_group, 'count': int(text_field)}

        self.set_status(f'working: {job.value.recipe}')
        print(self.set_recipe(job.value.recipe))

        worker()

        while self.output.count() != 0:
            self.set_status(f'output: {job.value.recipe}')
            sleep(5)

        #self.set_recipe(self.find_recipe(job.value.recipe))

        #fabricator.fabricator_main_order(self, need=job.value.count)
        #smart_fabric(self, job.value.recipe, job.value.count)

.transmission: list[str]
Искажённая передача: список токенов из одного символа каждый.

.analyzer: Analyzer
Восстановленный анализатор: сводит группу из пяти токенов к одному исходному токену, из которого они были развёрнуты.

    .read(group: list[str]) → str
    Прочитай список ровно из пяти строковых токенов и верни один токен, из которого они были развёрнуты. Аргумент, не являющийся списком, или элемент, не являющийся строкой, вызывает TypeError; неверная длина или нераспознанная группа вызывает ValueError.



.layers: int
Целое число слоёв пятикратного развёртывания, применённых к передаче.

def reagent(analyze):

    print('reagent')

    recipe = analyze.info.required_recipe
    # {'alkaline_buffer': 1, 'cryo_solvent': 2}

    for reagent in recipe.keys():
        n_need = recipe[reagent]
        while True:
            warehouse = self.input.connected_id()
            warehouse_comp = get_component(warehouse)
            if warehouse_comp.count(reagent) < n_need:
                print('need:', reagent)
                self.set_status(f'!!! need: {reagent}')
                sleep(10)
            else:
                self.set_status('work')
                break
        ioslots.input_take(self, reagent, n_need)
        self.load(reagent, n_need)

def work():

    print()

    self.set_status('output')
    while self.output.count() != 0:
        sleep(1)


    self.set_status('input')
    while not self.specimen:
        #take()
        print(self.take_from(collector))
        sleep(2)


    self.set_status('work')
    try:
        analyze = self.analyze()
        sleep(1)
        print(analyze)
        print(analyze.info.required_recipe)
    except Exception as e:
        print('err:', e)
        return

    # загрузка реагентов, проверка что они есть на сладе
    reagent(analyze)

    #print('extract')
    print(self.extract())

    self.clear_status()


def main():
    print('main go')
    #print(self.input.connect('warehouse_1'))
    #print(self.output.connect('bio_luminizer_1'))
    while True:
        work()

main()

drone_23.py×
import storages
import drones
import weather

clock = get_component("clock")

def main():

    drones.goto_station(self, 'drone_service_1')
    drones.oil_charging(self)
    drones.goto_station(self, 'drone_station_lrg_2')
    drones.cargo_unload(self)

    done_storm = []

    while True:

        st_raw = get_component('lead_cask_4')
        if st_raw.count('raw_uranium') > 70:
            print('storages full ...')
            sleep(20)
            continue

        if self.oil_tank.percent() < 0.3:
            drones.goto_station(self, 'drone_service_1')
            drones.oil_charging(self)

        coord = None

        while True:

            weather_station = get_component('weather_station_7')
            signals = weather_station.signal_receiver.transmissions()

            if signals:
                for signal in signals:
                    #print(signal)
                    #print(signal.data)
                    #print(signal.emitted_at_gh, clock.elapsed_game_hours(), signal.expires_at_gh)
                    data_split = signal.data.split('|')
                    #['storm_dust_496', '1', '8', '0', '0']
                    print(data_split)
                    if 'storm_dust' in data_split[0] and data_split[0] not in done_storm:
                    #print(signal.emitted_at_gh, clock.elapsed_game_hours(), signal.expires_at_gh)
                        signal_emitted = signal.emitted_at_gh
                        signal_expires = signal.expires_at_gh

                        coord = weather.get_dust_signals()
                        print(coord)
                        break


                if coord:
                    break

                print('skip all signal')
                sleep(20)

            else:
                print('no signals')
                sleep(20)

        drones.goto(self, coord[0], coord[1])

        while True:
            res = self.collect()
            print(res.status, signal_emitted, clock.elapsed_game_hours(), signal_expires)
            if res.status == 'ok':

                while True:
                    last_res = self.collect()
                    if last_res.status != 'ok':
                        break
                    sleep(3)

                drones.goto_station(self, 'drone_station_lrg_2')
                drones.cargo_unload(self)

                done_storm.append(data_split[0])

                print('done_storm len', len(done_storm))
                print()
                break

            sleep(10)

main()

import time

power = get_component("power_control")
run_control = get_component("run_control")

device_check = ['temp_heater', 'oxygen_generator', 'pressure_generator']

'''
outpost_1 pressure_generator pressure_6 1
outpost_1 pressure_generator pressure_7 1
outpost_2 oxygen_generator o2gen_11 2
outpost_2 oxygen_generator o2gen_12 2
'''

def collector(data: dict):
  for grid in power.grids():
    #print(grid.anchor_id, grid.generated, grid.consumed, grid.net)
    for member in grid.members:
        if member.type_id in device_check:
            _device_tier = get_component(member.id).tier()

            #print(member.outpost_id, member.type_id, member.id, _device_tier)

            if member.type_id in data:
                _count = data[member.type_id].get(_device_tier)
                if _count:
                    data[member.type_id][_device_tier] = _count + 1
                else:
                    data[member.type_id][_device_tier] = 1
            else:
                data[member.type_id] = {_device_tier: 1}

            #value = data.setdefault(member.type_id, {})
            #if data.get(member.type_id)


        #oxygen_generator: {1: 6, 2:2, 3:0}

def show(data):
    #print(data)

    #for i in data:
        #print(i, data[i])

    name_w = max(len(name) for name in data)
    cols = sorted({k for row in data.values() for k in row})  # все номера колонок: [1, 2]

    for name, row in data.items():
        cells = " | ".join(f"{row.get(c, ''):>2}" for c in cols)
        print(f"{name:<{name_w}} | {cells} |")


def main():
    while True:

        data = {}
        collector(data)

        #name_w = max(len(name) for name in data)

import storages
import ioslots
import random

def worker(self, count):

    print('WORKER')
    print('LOAD')
    for req, n in self.get_recipe_inputs().items():
        print(self.input.connect(storages.get_storages(req)))
        #print('take', req, n, '*', count)
        #print(self.input.take(req, n * count))
        ioslots.input_take(self, req, n * count)

    print('')
    print('RUNNING')
    while self.is_running():
        print('is_running sleep ...')
        sleep(3)

    print('')
    print('SEND')
    for mat in self.output.stacks():
        print(self.output.connect(storages.get_storages(mat.id)))
        #print('send', mat.id, mat.count)
        #print(self.output.send(mat.id, mat.count))
        ioslots.output_send(self, mat.id, mat.count)

    print('')
    print('END')


def smelter_auto(self):

    max_count = 1000
    types_id = ['iron_ingot', 'glass', 'titanium_ingot', 'cobalt_ingot', 'rare_earth_core', 'neutronium_bar', 'lead_ingot']
    max_capacity = self.output.capacity()

    ioslots.clear_input(self)
    ioslots.clear_output(self)
    self.clear_recipe()

    while True:
        #for type_id in types_id:

        type_id = random.choice(types_id)

        #print('smelt_' + type_id)

        storage_id = storages.get_storages(type_id)
        storage_component = get_component(storage_id)



        _condition = storage_component.count(type_id) < max_count
        print(type_id, _condition)

        if _condition:

            ioslots.clear_input(self)
            ioslots.clear_output(self)

            print(self.set_recipe('smelt_' + type_id))
            worker(self, max_capacity)

            ioslots.clear_input(self)
            ioslots.clear_output(self)

            self.clear_recipe()

        sleep(5)
    #out_storage_comp = get_component(out_storage)

core_sample.py×
from itertools import product


device = self.contract.device
#result = device.submit(index, bytes)
#print(result.status, result.message)


def solve(core):
    L = len(core)
    N = (L - 4) // 4
    assert 4 + 4 * N == L and N >= 2 and N % 2 == 0
    cell = lambda i, j: core[3 + 4 * i + j]          # j: 0 KIND, 1 LOAD, 2 PAIR, 3 SUM
    for v, want in ((core[0], 42), (core[1], N), (core[2], L)):
        assert v is None or v == want                 # заголовок: MARK, N, SPAN

    sols = []
    kind = [None] * N
    pair = [None] * N

    def finish():
        # для каждой ячейки: множество допустимых (LOAD -> SUM)
        opts = []
        for i in range(N):
            k, p = kind[i], pair[i]
            ld, sm = cell(i, 1), cell(i, 3)
            loads = [ld] if ld is not None else range(256)
            o = {}
            for l in loads:
                s = (k + l + p) % 256
                if sm is None or sm == s:
                    o.setdefault(s, l)
            if not o:
                return
            opts.append(o)
        # достижимые XOR всех SUM
        reach = [{0: None}]
        for o in opts:
            nxt = {}
            for x in reach[-1]:
                for s in o:
                    nxt.setdefault(x ^ s, (x, s))
            reach.append(nxt)
        lock = core[-1]
        targets = [lock] if lock is not None else list(reach[-1])
        for t in targets:
            if t in reach[-1]:
                x, loads, sums = t, [], []
                for i in range(N, 0, -1):
                    px, s = reach[i][x]
                    sums.append(s); loads.append(opts[i - 1][s]); x = px
                sums.reverse(); loads.reverse()
                out = [42, N, L]
                for i in range(N):
                    out += [kind[i], loads[i], pair[i], sums[i]]
                out.append(t)
                sols.append(out)

    def rec(i):
        if i == N:
            return finish()
        if pair[i] is not None:                       # уже связана ранее
            return rec(i + 1)
        ks = [cell(i, 0)] if cell(i, 0) is not None else [1, 2, 3, 4]
        for j in range(i + 1, N):
            if pair[j] is not None:
                continue
            for k in ks:
                if not 1 <= k <= 4: continue
                kj = 5 - k
                if cell(j, 0) not in (None, kj): continue
                if cell(i, 2) not in (None, j) or cell(j, 2) not in (None, i): continue
                kind[i], kind[j], pair[i], pair[j] = k, kj, j, i
                rec(i + 1)
                kind[i] = kind[j] = pair[i] = pair[j] = None

    rec(0)
    # ячейки, у которых известный PAIR указывает "назад", уже учтены через pair[j]
    return sols

for idx, core in enumerate(self.contract.cores):
    s = solve(core)
    assert len(s) == 1, (idx, len(s))
    r = device.submit(idx, bytes(s[0]))
    print(idx, r.status, r.message)

if device.recovered() == device.target():
    transmitter = get_component("transmitter")
    transmitter.connect("earth")
    transmitter.transmit(self.contract.id, device.token())

def update(data):
    # причать словарь и добавить новый, оператор |
    atmo = notebook.get("atmo") | data
    print(notebook.set("atmo", atmo))

#update()

def make_data():
    data = {str(clock.get_day()): [
        atmo.get_co2(),
        atmo.get_o2(),
        atmo.get_n2(),
        atmo.get_pressure(),
        atmo.get_temperature(),
        atmo.get_heat()
    ]}
    return data


#print(make_data())

        if not notebook.get("atmo").has(day):

        sleep(60)

main()

automation_2.py×
import time


power = get_component("power_control")


turnoff_limit = 20  # проценты
turnoon_limit = 70

device_check = ['temp_heater', 'oxygen_generator', 'pressure_generator']


def switcher(grid, switch):
    # False выключить
    # True включить

    for member in grid.members:
        if member.type_id in device_check:
            #print(member.id)

            if power.is_powered(member.id) != switch:
                # если нужно поменять состояние
                power.set_powered(member.id, switch)
                print(time.pretty_time_now(), grid.anchor_id, member.id, 'on' if switch else 'off')

def main():
    while True:
        for grid in power.grids():
            #print(grid.anchor_id, grid.generated, grid.consumed, grid.net)

            if grid.capacity == 0:
                continue

            if grid.stored == 0:
                switcher(grid, False)

            percent = grid.stored / grid.capacity * 100

            #print(grid.anchor_id, grid.stored, grid.capacity, percent)

            if percent < turnoff_limit:
                switcher(grid, False)

            if percent >= turnoon_limit:
                switcher(grid, True)
        sleep(10)

main()

    #for member in grid.members:
        #print("  ", member.id, member.roles, member.generated, member.consumed)

def show_board():
    board = arbiter.board()
    line = ''
    for n, cel in enumerate(board):
        if cel == 'arbiter': line += '[0]'
        elif cel == 'you': line += '[X]'
        elif cel == '': line += '[ ]'

        if n == 2:
            print(line, '             ')
            line = ''
        elif n == 5:
            print(line, '               ')
            line = ''
    print(line)
    print()

#   0 1 2
#   3 4 5
#   6 7 8

#[ ][ ][0]
#[ ][X][ ]
#[ ][ ][ ]

#['arbiter', '', '', '', 'you', '', '', '', '']

def play_tactics():
        show = False

        arbiter.play(0)
        if show: show_board()

        arbiter.play(8)
        if show: show_board()

        if arbiter.board()[6] == 'arbiter':
            arbiter.play(2)
            if show: show_board()

            if arbiter.board()[1] == 'arbiter':
                arbiter.play(5)
                if show: show_board()
            else:
                arbiter.play(1)
                if show: show_board()

        elif arbiter.board()[2] == 'arbiter':
            arbiter.play(6)
            if show: show_board()

            if arbiter.board()[3] == 'arbiter':
                arbiter.play(7)
                if show: show_board()
            else:
                arbiter.play(3)
                if show: show_board()

def main():
    while True:
        print()
        print('===================================')
        arbiter.new_game()

        play_tactics()

        streak = arbiter.streak()
        target = arbiter.target()

        print(streak, '/', target)

        if streak == target:
            transmitter = get_component("transmitter")
            transmitter.connect("earth")
            transmitter.transmit(self.contract.id, arbiter.token())
            break
        #sleep(1)

main()

import ioslots



#print(self.output.connect('bio_exchange_6'))

def dnk():
    self.load(self.input.stacks()[0].id)

    have_genes = self.genes()
    bio_exchange = get_component('bio_exchange_6')
    #need_genes = bio_exchange.active_order().required_genes[self.chamber.fragment_id]
    order = bio_exchange.active_order()
    need_genes = order.required_genes[order.required_genes.keys()[0]]

    gene_for_splice = []

    for gene in need_genes:
        if gene not in have_genes:
            gene_for_splice.append(gene)

    print(have_genes)
    print(need_genes)
    print(gene_for_splice)

    self.splice(gene_for_splice)

    ioslots.clear_output(self)


def main():
    while True:
        if self.input.count() != 0:
            dnk()
        else:
            sleep(1)

main()

def reagent(analyze):

    print('reagent')

    recipe = analyze.info.required_recipe
    # {'alkaline_buffer': 1, 'cryo_solvent': 2}

    for reagent in recipe.keys():
        n_need = recipe[reagent]
        ioslots.input_take(self, reagent, n_need)
        self.load(reagent, n_need)

        take()

drone_20.py×
import storages
import drones
import weather


def main():

    drones.goto_station(self, 'drone_service_1')
    drones.oil_charging(self)
    drones.goto_station(self, 'drone_station_lrg_1')
    drones.cargo_unload(self)


    while True:

        if self.oil_tank.percent() < 0.3:
            drones.goto_station(self, 'drone_service_1')
            drones.oil_charging(self)

        while True:
            coord = weather.get_lightning_signals()
            if coord:
                print(coord)
                break
            sleep(10)

        drones.goto(self, coord[0], coord[1])

        while True:
            res = self.collect()
            if res.status == 'ok':
                self.collect()
                drones.goto_station(self, 'drone_station_lrg_1')
                drones.cargo_unload(self)
                break
            sleep(10)

main()

bio_collector_1.py×

bio_exchange_1 = get_component("bio_exchange_1")
sites = self.scan()




def need_req(active_order):
    # что и сколько осталось выкопать

    need_req = {}

    for req, n in active_order.delivered.items():
        need_req[req] = active_order.requires[req] - n

    print('need_req', need_req)

    return need_req

def go_for(req, value):
    # погнали копать

    print('go_for', req, value)

    for site in sites:
        if req == site.fragment_id:
            site_coords = site.coords
            break

    print(' site_coords', site_coords)

    # копка, одажние освобождения
    for n in range(value):
        rdy = False
        while not rdy:
            #print(1)
            if not self.cargo:
                #print(2)
                self.collect(site.coords)
                rdy = True
                print(' collect', n + 1, '/', value)

def main():
    print('main')

    active_order_id_done = ''

    while True:
        active_order = bio_exchange_1.active_order()

        if not active_order or active_order.id == active_order_id_done:
            sleep(10)
            print('sleep ...')
            continue

        print(active_order.id)
        #print(active_order.requires)

        _need_req = need_req(active_order)

        for req, value in _need_req.items():
            #if value > 0:

            go_for(req, value)

        active_order_id_done = active_order.id
        print('active_order_id_done', active_order_id_done)
main()

def reagent(analyze):

    print('reagent')

    recipe = analyze.info.required_recipe
    # {'alkaline_buffer': 1, 'cryo_solvent': 2}

    for reagent in recipe.keys():
        n_need = recipe[reagent]
        shop.buy(reagent, quantity=n_need)
        self.input.take(reagent, n_need)
        self.load(reagent, n_need)

        print(' load', reagent, n_need)

    #print(self.input.connected_to())
    #self.input(reagent, n_need)
    #if loaded < n_need:
    #    self.load(reagent, n_need - loaded)


def main():

    print('main go')

    while True:

        if not self.specimen:
            take()
        analyze = self.analyze()
        #print(type(analyze))
        #print(analyze.info.required_recipe)


        # для экстракта
        #loaded = self.loaded_reagents.get(reagent, 0)
        #print(self.loaded_reagents)

        reagent(analyze)

        print('extract')
        self.extract()

        #отправить все в

import storages
import storages_exotic
import ioslots

#print(self.list_recipes())

#for i in self.list_recipes():
    #print(i)

#self.set_recipe(recipe_id)



def refinering(self, exotics):

    # смола
    print(self.input.connect('large_warehouse_13'))

    for exotic in exotics:
        print(exotic)
        self.set_recipe(exotic)
        active_recipe = self.find_recipe(exotic)

        if 'gas_in' in active_recipe.fluid_inputs:
            # газ
            print(self.gas_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[active_recipe.input_fluid]))
            print(self.gas_out.connect(storages_exotic.container[active_recipe.output_fluid]))

        else:
            # жидкость
            print(self.liquid_in.connect(storages_exotic.container[active_recipe.input_fluid]))
            print(self.liquid_out.connect(storages_exotic.container[active_recipe.output_fluid]))

        output_container_id = storages_exotic.container[active_recipe.output_fluid]
        output_container_comp = get_component(output_container_id)

        #output_fill_pct = output_container_comp.fill_pct()
        #output_level = output_container_comp.level()

        while (output_level := output_container_comp.level()) < 800:

            print('output_level', output_container_id, output_level)
            self.set_status(f'{output_container_id}: {output_level}/{output_container_comp.capacity()}')
            ioslots.input_take(self, 'tar', active_recipe.inputs['tar'] * 3)

            while self.is_running():
                sleep(1)

            output_level = output_container_comp.level()


        self.clear_recipe()
        print('done')
        sleep(5)



def main(self, exotics):
    #print(self.input.flush())

    while True:
        refinering(self, exotics)
        sleep(10)

import auto
import storages
import ioslots
import storages_frozen


BIOMES = {
    'прибрежный': [
        ['salt_crust', 'tide_moss'],
        ['sea_algae', 'shore_lichen'],
        ['coral_fungus', 'brine_plankton']
    ],
    'вулканы': [
        ['ash_spores', 'lava_algae'],
        ['cinder_lichen', 'magma_crust'],
        ['black_fungus', 'sulfur_moss']
    ],
    'глубинный': [
        ['cave_moss', 'stone_lichen'],
        ['crystal_spores', 'cave_fungus'],
        ['deep_algae', 'stone_mat']
    ],
    'геотермал': [
        ['heat_lichen', 'hot_spores'],
        ['vent_algae', 'steam_moss'],
        ['heat_crust', 'vent_fungus']
    ]
}


def road_for_life(self, biom_name):

    '''

    сменить:
    biom =
    coord_x, coord_y  =
    self.input.connect(storages.coastal[item])

    '''
    count = 450

    for hodka_za_parou_items in BIOMES[biom_name]:
        print()
        print('-------')
        print(hodka_za_parou_items)

        auto.move(self, 0, 0)
        auto.battery_charging(self)

        coord_x, coord_y  = get_component_by_name(biom_name).coords()
        auto.move(self, coord_x, coord_y)

        for item in hodka_za_parou_items:
            print(item)
            self.input.connect(storages.biom_lifeforms[biom_name])
            ioslots.input_take(self, item, count)

        auto.move(self, 0, 0)

        for item in hodka_za_parou_items:
            print(item)
            self.output.connect(storages_frozen.lifes[item])
            ioslots.output_send(self, item, count)

        auto.battery_charging(self)

#result = tablet.probe(14, 4)

#tablet.rows / tablet.cols — размеры сетки


#row: int, col: int
print(tablet.probe(0, 0).char, '|', tablet.probe(0, 1).char, '|', tablet.probe(0, 2).char)
print(tablet.probe(1, 0).char, '|', tablet.probe(1, 1).char, '|', tablet.probe(1, 2).char)
print()

def perebor():

    message: str = ''

    for r in range(30):
        for c in range(30):

            result = tablet.probe(r, c)
            #print(result)
            if result.distance == 0:
                print(r, c, result.char, result.distance)
                message += result.char
    return message


message = perebor()




print(message)

transmitter = get_component("transmitter")
transmitter.connect("earth")
transmitter.transmit(self.contract.id, message)

        #ioslots.clear_input(self)

bio_exchange_1 = get_component("bio_exchange_2")
sites = self.scan()




def need_req(active_order):
    # что и сколько осталось выкопать

    need_req = {}

    for req, n in active_order.delivered.items():
        need_req[req] = active_order.requires[req] - n

    print('need_req', need_req)

    return need_req

def go_for(req, value):
    # погнали копать

    print('go_for', req, value)

    for site in sites:
        if req == site.fragment_id:
            site_coords = site.coords
            break

    print(' site_coords', site_coords)

    # копка, одажние освобождения
    for n in range(value):
        rdy = False
        while not rdy:
            #print(1)
            if not self.cargo:
                #print(2)
                self.collect(site.coords)
                rdy = True
                print(' collect', n + 1, '/', value)

def main():
    print('main')

    active_order_id_done = ''

bio_collector_5.py×

bio_exchange_1 = get_component("bio_exchange_5")
sites = self.scan()


def need_req(active_order):
    # что и сколько осталось выкопать

    need_req = {}

    for req, n in active_order.delivered.items():
        need_req[req] = active_order.requires[req] - n

    print('need_req', need_req)

    return need_req

def go_for(req, value):
    # погнали копать

    print('go_for', req, value)

    for site in sites:
        if req == site.fragment_id:
            site_coords = site.coords
            break

    print(' site_coords', site_coords)

    # копка, одажние освобождения
    for n in range(value):
        rdy = False
        while not rdy:
            #print(1)
            if not self.cargo:
                #print(2)
                self.collect(site.coords)
                rdy = True
                print(' collect', n + 1, '/', value)

def main():
    print('main')

    active_order_id_done = ''

    while True:
        active_order = bio_exchange_1.active_order()

        if not active_order or active_order.id == active_order_id_done:
            sleep(10)
            print('sleep ...')
            continue

        print(active_order.id)
        #print(active_order.requires)

        _need_req = need_req(active_order)

        for req, value in _need_req.items():
            #if value > 0:

            go_for(req, value)
            print('sleep(15)')
            sleep(15)

        active_order_id_done = active_order.id
        print('active_order_id_done', active_order_id_done)

main()

def main(self):

    power = get_component("power_control")
    grid = power.grid(self.name)

    print(self.steam_in.connect('gas_tank_1'))

    while True:

        ratio = grid.stored/grid.capacity

        if ratio < 0.3:
            self.set_throttle(1)
        elif ratio <= 0.6:
            self.set_throttle(0.5)
        else:
            self.set_throttle(0)
        #print(grid)
        #stored=185.6337583486654, capacity=1000
        #print(grid.stored/grid.capacity)
        print(ratio, self.throttle())
        sleep(2)

    tank_steam_id = storages.storages['steam']
    tank_water_id = storages.storages['water']

    print(tank_steam_id)
    print(tank_water_id)

    print(self.steam_in.connect(tank_steam_id))
    print(self.water_out.connect(tank_water_id))

    tank_steam_comp = get_component(tank_steam_id)
    tank_water_comp = get_component(tank_water_id)

    while True:
        while tank_steam_comp.fill_pct() > 0.3 and tank_water_comp.fill_pct() < 0.8:
            print(self.status(), 'пар:', tank_steam_comp.fill_pct(), 'вода:', tank_water_comp.fill_pct(), '| конденсация в час:', self.condensation_rate())
            self.set_throttle(1)
            sleep(2)
        self.set_throttle(0)
        sleep(2)

bio_exchange_5.py×
import ioslots

def set_order(biom):

    orders = self.orders()

    _set_order_ok = False


    for order in orders:

        #if order.id == 'bio_order_72':
            #raise Exception('bio_order_72')

        if order.biome == biom and order.status=='available':
            self.set_order(order.id)
            print('set_order', order.id)
            _set_order_ok = True
            break

    if not _set_order_ok:
        raise Exception('NO ORDER!')

def main():

    #bioms: 'frozen' 'coastal' 'geothermal' 'volcanic' 'deep'

    biom = 'volcanic'

    print(self.input.connect('bio_caster_2'))
    bio_caster_2 = get_component('bio_caster_2')

    while True:

        if not self.active_order():
            set_order(biom)

        self.set_status('input')
        while self.input.count() == 0:
            if bio_caster_2.output.stacks():
                ioslots.input_take(self, bio_caster_2.output.stacks()[0].id, 1)
                break
            else:
                sleep(1)

        self.set_status('work')
        print(self.deliver())


main()

# с 1 три правильных

def main():

    for position in range(15):
        for n in range(2, 6):

            result = terminal.guess([1 for i in range(15)])
            good_place_count = result.correct

            code_test = [1 for i in range(15)]
            code_test[position] = n
            print(code_test)
            result = terminal.guess(code_test)

            if n == 2 and result.correct < good_place_count:
                #code[position] = 1
                break
                print(code, position, 1)
            if result.correct > good_place_count:
                code[position] = n
                print(code, position, n)
                break
    print(code)
