import ioslots


def set_order(biom):

    orders = self.orders()
    _set_order_ok = False

    for order in orders:
        #if order.id == 'bio_order_72':
            #raise Exception('bio_order_72')
        if order.biome == biom and order.status=='available':
            self.set_order(order.id)
            print('set_order', order.id)
            _set_order_ok = True
            break

    if not _set_order_ok:
        raise Exception('NO ORDER!')


def main():

    #bioms: 'frozen' 'coastal' 'geothermal' 'volcanic' 'deep'
    biom = 'deep'

    print(self.input.connect('bio_conditioner_1'))
    input_device = get_component('bio_conditioner_1')

    while True:

        if not self.active_order():
            set_order(biom)

        self.set_status('input')
        while self.input.count() == 0:
            if input_device.output.stacks():
                ioslots.input_take(self, input_device.output.stacks()[0].id, 1)
                break
            else:
                sleep(1)

        self.set_status('work')
        print(self.deliver())


main()

drone_service_1.py×
import storages

print(self.oil_in.connect(storages.storages['oil']))

def charge():
    get_docked = self.get_docked()
    #print(get_docked)
    for drone in get_docked:
        #print(type(car))

        drone_comp = get_component(drone)

        try:
            print(drone_comp.battery.capacity())
            engine = "electric"
        except ReferenceError:
            print('no battery')
            engine = "heli"

        if engine == "electric" and drone_comp.battery.percent() != 1:
            print('charge', drone)
            self.charge(drone, 1.0)
        if engine == "heli" and drone_comp.oil_tank.percent() != 1:
            print('refuel', drone)
            self.refuel(drone, 1.0)


#.refuel(drone_id: str, target_level: float = 1.0) → ActionResult

def rescue():
    #outposts = get_component("outpost_network").outposts()
    drones = get_component("fleet").drones()
    for drone in drones:
        if not drone.is_docked and drone.battery_level == 0:
            print('rescue', drone.id)
            self.dispatch_rescue(drone.id, 0.2)

def main():
    while True:

        charge()
        rescue()
        sleep(3)

main()

import storages

def main():

    tank_steam_id = storages.storages['steam']
    tank_water_id = storages.storages['water']

    print(tank_steam_id)
    print(tank_water_id)

    print(self.steam_in.connect(tank_steam_id))
    print(self.water_out.connect(tank_water_id))

    tank_steam_comp = get_component(tank_steam_id)

corrupted_archive.py×
get_component("console").clear()

#сетку 10×10

archive = self.contract.archive
word = archive.flip(9, 9)
print(word)


print(archive.rows) #строки
print(archive.cols) #столбцы

check_done = []
pairs = []

def perebor():
    for r in range(10):
        for c in range(10):

            if [r, c] in check_done:
                continue

            word = archive.flip(r, c)

            for r2 in range(10):
                for c2 in range(10):

                    if r == r2 and c == c2:
                        continue

                    word2 = archive.flip(r2, c2)
                    if word == word2:
                        pairs.append([r, c, r2, c2])
                        check_done.append([r, c])
                        check_done.append([r2, c2])



perebor()
print(len(pairs))

#pairs = [[0, 0, 3, 7], [0, 1, 5, 2], ...]
transmitter = get_component("transmitter")
transmitter.connect("earth")
transmitter.transmit(self.contract.id, pairs)

'''
• archive.rows — число строк сетки
• archive.cols — число столбцов сетки

Найди все 50 совпадающих пар.
Отправь список элементов вида [row1, col1, row2, col2].

pairs = [[0, 0, 3, 7], [0, 1, 5, 2], ...]
transmitter = get_component("transmitter")
transmitter.connect("earth")
transmitter.transmit(self.contract.id, pairs)

'''
drifting_signal.py×
import string

#ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ
print(string.ascii_uppercase)
abc = string.ascii_uppercase

device = self.contract.device
print(device.slabs)

def next_slabs(slabs):

    next_slabs = ''

    for letter in slabs:
        #print(letter)
        if letter == ' ':
            next_slabs += ' '
            continue
        index = abc.find(letter)
        if letter == 'Z':
            next_slabs += 'A'
            continue
        else:
            next_letter = abc[index + 1]
        next_slabs += next_letter

    return next_slabs

'''

    print(_try)

charging_station_2.py×

def charge():
    get_docked = self.get_docked()
    for car in get_docked:
        #print(type(car))

        if get_component(car).battery.level() != 1:
            print('charge', car)
            self.charge(car, 1.0)


def rescue():
    #outposts = get_component("outpost_network").outposts()
    vehicles = get_component("fleet").vehicles()

    for car in vehicles:
        if car.docked_at != 'outpost_home' and car.battery_level == 0:

            #car_co = get_component("fleet")

            print('rescue', car.id)
            self.dispatch_rescue(car.id, 0.3)


def main():
    while True:

        charge()
        #rescue()
        sleep(5)

main()

drone_3.py×
import ioslots
import drones
import storages


clock = get_component("clock")


def get_clock():
    day = clock.get_day()
    h, m, s = clock.get_time()
    return f'{day}, {h:02d}:{m:02d}.{s:02d}'



def worker_tester():
    frozen_points = [60, 100]

    print()

    drones.goto_station(self, 'drone_station_2')
    while self.cargo.count() != 0:
        drones.cargo_unload(self)

    drones.goto_station(self, 'drone_service_1')
    drones.battery_charging(self)

    drones.goto(self, frozen_points[0], frozen_points[1])
    print(get_clock(), self.position())

    res = self.bio_extractor.extract()
    print(get_clock(), 'bio_extractor status', res.status, res)


def main():
    while True:
        worker_tester()

#main()

drones.goto(self, 70, 70)
